# Events for handling the Sailing Actitivy

namespace = agot_sailing

###########
## SETUP ##
###########

scripted_trigger has_available_guest = {
	scope:activity ?= {
		any_attending_character = {
			NOT = { this = scope:host }
			exists = this
		}
	}
}

scripted_effect save_capital_scope = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_landed = yes }
		root.capital_province = { save_scope_as = capital_province }
	}
	else = {
		root.domicile.domicile_location = { save_scope_as = capital_province }
	}
}

scripted_effect xp_modifiers = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			culture = { has_cultural_tradition = tradition_seafaring }
			has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent
		}
		add_trait_xp = {
			trait = lifestyle_mariner
			value = 10
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				culture = { has_cultural_tradition = tradition_seafaring }
				has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent
			}
		}
		add_trait_xp = {
			trait = lifestyle_mariner
			value = 6
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_trait_xp = {
			trait = lifestyle_mariner
			value = 3
		}
	}
}

# Arrive at the Docks / Setup
agot_sailing.0800 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.0800.t
	desc = agot_sailing.0800.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = { reference = docks }
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = happiness
	}
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:activity = {
					has_activity_option = {
						category = agot_sailing_option_ship_choice
						option = agot_sailing_charter
					}
				}
			}
			scope:host = {
				add_character_modifier = small_boat_modifier
				add_character_flag = hired_a_boat
			}
		}
		involved_activity.activity_location = {
			save_scope_as = location
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:activity = {
					has_activity_option = {
						category = special_type
						option = agot_sailing_type_valyria
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					culture = { has_cultural_parameter = more_frequent_maritime_hunts }
				}
				set_variable = {
					name = attempted_valyria
					years = 2
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = {
					name = attempted_valyria
					years = 5
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Let's get everything prepared!
		name = agot_sailing.0800.a

		trigger = {
			has_available_guest = yes
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					has_map_here = { LOCATION = scope:activity.activity_location }
					scope:activity.activity_location = { has_province_modifier = sunken_ship_sighted }
				}
				has_character_flag = treasure_hunt
				scope:activity = {
					has_activity_option = {
						category = special_type
						option = agot_sailing_type_treasure
					}
				}
			}
			invalidate_activity = scope:activity
			custom_tooltip = nice_try_tt
		}
		else = {
			trigger_event = {
				on_action = sailing_dockyard_events
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # No one came, can't man the ship
		name = agot_sailing.0800.b

		trigger = {
			has_available_guest = no
		}

		custom_tooltip = sailing_ends_tt
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = small_boat_modifier }
			remove_character_modifier = small_boat_modifier
		}
		invalidate_activity = scope:activity
	}
}

# 1005 - Dockyard temptations
# 1007 - Whittle While You Wait

agot_sailing.1005 = { #Temptation at the Docks
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.1005.t
	desc = agot_sailing.1005.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = { reference = docks }
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = happiness
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:hooker
		animation = flirtation
	}

	trigger = {
		is_eunuch_trigger = no
		NOR = {
			has_trait = celibate
			has_sexuality = asexual
			has_character_flag = had_event_sailing_1005
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		add_character_flag = {
			flag = had_event_sailing_1005
			years = 15
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			save_capital_scope = yes
			create_character = {
				location = scope:capital_province
				gender_female_chance = root_attraction_based_female_chance
				template = beautiful_peasant_character
				faith = scope:activity.activity_location.faith
				culture = scope:activity.activity_location.culture
				random_traits_list = {
					count = 1
					gregarious = {}
					lustful = {}
					compassionate = {}
					generous = {}
					ambitious = {}
				}
				save_scope_as = hooker
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				scope:hooker = {
					set_sexuality = bisexual
					set_to_lowborn = yes
				}
				random_list = {
					60 = {
						scope:hooker = { add_trait = seducer }
					}
					40 = {
						scope:hooker = { add_trait = lovers_pox }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	# Take as dock wench/boy
	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.1005.a
		trigger = {
			can_set_relation_lover_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:hooker }
		}
		set_relation_lover = scope:hooker
		had_sex_with_effect = {
			CHARACTER = scope:hooker
			PREGNANCY_CHANCE = pregnancy_chance
		}
		scope:activity = {
			add_activity_log_entry = {
				key = sailing_had_sexual_encounter
				tags = { good random social }
				# this line below adds the entry to the Effects section of the conclusion UI
				show_in_conclusion = yes
				character = root
				target = scope:hooker
				root = { remove_short_term_gold = 5 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { allowed_more_concubines = yes }
			scope:activity = {
				add_activity_log_entry = {
					key = sailing_gained_concubine_log
					tags = { good random social }
					# this line below adds the entry to the Effects section of the conclusion UI
					show_in_conclusion = yes
					character = root
					target = scope:hooker
					root = {
						make_concubine = scope:hooker
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random = {
				chance = 2
				trigger_event = agot_sailing.1006
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				has_trait = rakish
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				has_trait = seducer
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				has_trait = lustful
			}
			ai_value_modifier = {
				ai_compassion = -0.5
				ai_honor = -0.5
				ai_greed = 0.5
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				has_trait = chaste
			}
			modifier = {
				might_cheat_on_every_partner_trigger = no
				factor = 0
			}
		}
	}

	# Lay with them
	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.1005.b
		had_sex_with_effect = {
			CHARACTER = scope:hooker
			PREGNANCY_CHANCE = pregnancy_chance
		}
		scope:activity = {
			add_activity_log_entry = {
				key = sailing_had_sexual_encounter
				tags = { good random social }
				# this line below adds the entry to the Effects section of the conclusion UI
				show_in_conclusion = yes
				character = root
				target = scope:hooker
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				2 = {
					trigger_event = agot_sailing.1006
				}
				98 = {
					scope:hooker = { silent_disappearance_effect = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				has_trait = rakish
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				has_trait = seducer
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				has_trait = lustful
			}
			ai_value_modifier = {
				ai_compassion = -0.5
				ai_honor = -0.5
				ai_greed = 0.5
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				has_trait = chaste
			}
			modifier = {
				might_cheat_on_every_partner_trigger = no
				factor = 0
			}
		}
	}

	# Refuse
	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.1005.c
		add_piety = 20
		stress_impact = {
			chaste = minor_stress_impact_loss
			lustful = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			scope:hooker = { silent_disappearance_effect = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				add = -100
				has_trait = rakish
			}
			modifier = {
				add = -100
				has_trait = seducer
			}
			modifier = {
				add = -100
				has_trait = lustful
			}
			ai_value_modifier = {
				ai_compassion = 0.5
				ai_honor = 0.5
				ai_greed = -0.5
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				has_trait = chaste
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.1990
			days = 15
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.1006 = { #A Dagger in the Bedroom
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.1006.t
	desc = agot_sailing.1006.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = bedchamber
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = fear
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:hooker
		animation = scheme
	}

	option = { # A knife!
		name = agot_sailing.1006.a
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.1006.a.desc
		duel = {
			skill = prowess
			value = very_high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 1
					min = -19
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.1006.duel_success
				add_stress = massive_stress_gain
				imprison = {
					target = scope:hooker
					type = dungeon
				}
			}
			100 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -1
					min = -19
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.1006.duel_failure
				death = {
					killer = scope:hooker
					death_reason = death_murder
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.1007 = { #Whittle While You Wait
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.1007.t
	desc = agot_sailing.1007.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = { reference = docks }
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = boredom
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:whittler
		animation = personality_compassionate
	}

	trigger = {
		has_available_guest = yes
		scope:activity = {
			any_attending_character = {
				is_alive = yes
				is_ai = yes
				NOT = { this = scope:host }
			}
			exists = this
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		scope:activity = {
			random_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					is_alive = yes
					is_ai = yes
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				save_scope_as = whittler
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Make something for my child
		name = agot_sailing.1007.a
		trigger = {
			any_child = { age < 16 }
		}
		random = {
			chance = 5
			increase_wounds_no_death_effect = { REASON = accident }
		}
		random_child = {
			limit = { age < 16 }
			add_opinion = {
				modifier = impressed_opinion
				target = root
				opinion = 10
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			diligent = medium_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				has_trait = diligent
				add = 20
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Make something for my lover
		name = agot_sailing.1007.b
		trigger = {
			num_of_relation_lover > 0
		}
		random = {
			chance = 5
			increase_wounds_no_death_effect = { REASON = accident }
		}
		random_relation = {
			type = lover
			add_opinion = {
				modifier = impressed_opinion
				target = root
				opinion = 10
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			diligent = medium_stress_impact_loss
			lazy = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				has_trait = diligent
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = lazy
				add = 20
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Make a flute
		name = agot_sailing.1007.c
		trigger = {
			has_trait = lifestyle_poet
		}
		random = {
			chance = 10
			increase_wounds_no_death_effect = { REASON = accident }
		}
		add_character_modifier = handcarved_flute
		stress_impact = {
			diligent = medium_stress_impact_loss
			lazy = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				has_trait = diligent
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = lazy
				add = 20
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Make something trivial
		name = agot_sailing.1007.d
		random = {
			chance = 5
			increase_wounds_no_death_effect = { REASON = accident }
		}
		stress_impact = {
			base = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
		}
	}

	option = { # I'd rather not...
		name = agot_sailing.1007.e
		add_prestige = miniscule_prestige_gain
		stress_impact = {
			diligent = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				has_trait = diligent
				add = -20
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.1990
			days = 15
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.1008 = { #Crewmates in a Bar Brawl
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.1008.t
	desc = agot_sailing.1008.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = tavern
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = personality_bold
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:brawler
		animation = anger
	}

	trigger = {
		has_available_guest = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		scope:activity = {
			random_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					is_alive = yes
					is_ai = yes
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				save_scope_as = brawler
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Try to diffuse the situation
		name = agot_sailing.1008.a
		duel = {
			skill = diplomacy
			value = 15
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.1008.a.success
				add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
				reverse_add_opinion = {
					target = scope:brawler
					modifier = respect_opinion
					opinion = low_positive_opinion
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.1008.a.failure
				add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
				increase_wounds_effect = { REASON = fight }
				reverse_add_opinion = {
					target = scope:brawler
					modifier = weak_opinion
					opinion = low_negative_opinion
				}
				scope:brawler = {
					increase_wounds_effect = { REASON = fight }
				}
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			shy = medium_stress_impact_gain
			callous = minor_stress_impact_gain
			sadistic = medium_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				diplomacy >= 15
				add = 30
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = diplomat
				add = 30
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = brave
				add = -30
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = craven
				add = -30
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = shy
				add = -30
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = callous
				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = sadistic
				add = -20
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Join in the brawl
		name = agot_sailing.1008.b
		duel = {
			skill = prowess
			value = 15
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 4
					min = -49
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.1008.b.success
				add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
				reverse_add_opinion = {
					target = scope:brawler
					modifier = respect_opinion
					opinion = medium_positive_opinion
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -4
					min = -49
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.1008.b.failure
				add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
				increase_wounds_effect = { REASON = fight }
				reverse_add_opinion = {
					target = scope:brawler
					modifier = respect_opinion
					opinion = low_positive_opinion
				}
				scope:brawler = {
					increase_wounds_effect = { REASON = fight }
				}
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			#Rare opportunity for certain character types to 'blow off some steam' and lose stress here, at risk of wounds.
			wrathful = medium_stress_loss
			vengeful = medium_stress_loss
			sadistic = medium_stress_loss

			craven = major_stress_impact_gain
			calm = medium_stress_impact_gain
			patient = minor_stress_impact_gain
			forgiving = minor_stress_impact_gain
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				has_trait = brave
				add = 30
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = craven
				add = -30
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = shy
				add = -30
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = wrathful
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = vengeful
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = sadistic
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = calm
				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = patient
				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = forgiving
				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = compassionate
				add = -20
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Leave them to it
		name = agot_sailing.1008.c
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_loss
		reverse_add_opinion = {
			target = scope:brawler
			modifier = refusal_opinion
			opinion = high_negative_opinion
		}
		scope:brawler = {
			increase_wounds_effect = { REASON = fight }
		}
		stress_impact = {
			drunkard = medium_stress_impact_loss
			diligent = minor_stress_impact_gain
			just = minor_stress_impact_gain
			reclusive = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				has_trait = drunkard
				add = 30
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = diligent
				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = just
				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = reclusive
				add = -20
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.1990
			days = 15
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.1009 = { #The Drinking Contest
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.1009.t
	desc = agot_sailing.1009.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = tavern
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = dismissal
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:challenger
		animation = personality_bold
	}

	trigger = {
		has_available_guest = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		scope:activity = {
			random_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					is_alive = yes
					is_ai = yes
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				save_scope_as = challenger
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # You're on!
		name = agot_sailing.1009.a
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				desc = agot_sailing.1009.a.success
				add_gold = 25
				add_prestige = 50
				reverse_add_opinion = {
					target = scope:challenger
					modifier = respect_opinion
					opinion = low_positive_opinion
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 40
					has_trait = drunkard
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 20
					has_trait = lifestyle_reveler

						modifier = bad_omen_modifier
						years = 2
					}

			}

			}

				}

			}
		}

			base = 30
		}
	}

	option = { # Have them whipped
		name = agot_sailing.1012.b
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:laugher = { highest_held_title_tier > tier_barony }

			add_dread = medium_dread_gain
			set_relation_rival = scope:laugher
		}
		else = {
			add_dread = minor_dread_gain
		}
		scope:laugher = {
			increase_wounds_effect = { REASON = whipping }
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = angry_opinion
				opinion = medium_negative_opinion
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			compassionate = medium_stress_impact_gain
			calm = minor_stress_impact_gain
			sadistic = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				has_trait = sadistic
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = vengeful
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = compassionate
				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = calm
				add = -20
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.1990
			days = 15
		}
	}

agot_sailing.1013 = { #An Experienced Sailor
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.1013.t
	desc = agot_sailing.1013.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = { reference = docks }
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = personality_honorable
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:recruit
		animation = personality_cynical
	}

	immediate = {
		save_capital_scope = yes
		create_character = {
			location = scope:capital_province
			gender = male
			template = new_commander_character
			age = 36
			faith = scope:activity.activity_location.faith
			culture = scope:activity.activity_location.culture
			trait = lifestyle_mariner
			save_scope_as = recruit
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:recruit.culture = culture:high_valyrian }
			scope:recruit = { set_culture = culture:westerosi_valyrian }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			scope:recruit ?= {
				add_trait_xp = {
					trait = lifestyle_mariner
					value = { 25 75 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Diplomacy - Convince them to join
		name = agot_sailing.1013.a
		duel = {
			skill = diplomacy
			value = 12
			60 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 20
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 30
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					has_access_to_a_small_ship = yes
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 20
					has_access_to_a_big_ship = yes
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.1013.a.success
				send_interface_toast = {
					title = agot_sailing.1013.a.success
					add_courtier = scope:recruit
					if = {
						limit = {
							scope:recruit = { can_join_activity = scope:activity }
						}
						scope:recruit = { add_to_activity = scope:activity }
					}
					reverse_add_opinion = {
						target = scope:recruit
						modifier = respect_opinion
						opinion = low_positive_opinion
					}
				}
			}
			40 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.1013.a.failure
				send_interface_toast = {
					title = agot_sailing.1013.a.failure
					add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
					hidden_effect = {
						scope:recruit = {
							silent_disappearance_effect = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				diplomacy >= 12
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = shy
				add = -20
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Bribe - Pay the to join
		name = agot_sailing.1013.b
		add_courtier = scope:recruit
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recruit = { can_join_activity = scope:activity }
			}
			scope:recruit = { add_to_activity = scope:activity }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recruit = {
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}
			}
			remove_short_term_gold = 100
		}
		else = {
			remove_short_term_gold = 50
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				has_trait = greedy
				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				short_term_gold < 150
				add = -20
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Refuse - Choose not to recruit
		name = agot_sailing.1013.c
		hidden_effect = {
			scope:recruit = {
				silent_disappearance_effect = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
		}
	}

	after = {
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.1990
			days = 15
		}
	}
}

			base = 30

				short_term_gold < 10
				add = -20
			}
		}
	}

			90 = { add_stress = -20 }

				add_stress = -30

					limit = {
						NOT = { has_trait = drunkard }
					}
					add_trait = drunkard
				}
			}
		}
		remove_short_term_gold = 30
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				has_trait = temperate
				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = drunkard
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {

			}
		}
	}

				}
				agot_sunken_treasure_map_effect = { OWNER = root }
			}
			95 = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					left_icon = root
					title = agot_sailing.1014.d.tt.failure.toast
				}
				add_stress = minor_stress_gain
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				has_trait = greedy
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = ambitious
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = paranoid
				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				short_term_gold < 250
				add = -40
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Refuse - I don't need anything
		name = agot_sailing.1014.e
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
		}
	}

	after = {
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.1990
			days = 15
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.1015 = { #The Old Beggar
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.1015.t
	desc = agot_sailing.1015.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = docks
	}
	center_portrait = {
		character = scope:dockyard_guest
		animation = anger
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:dockyard_beggar
		animation = obsequious_bow
	}
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:activity = {
					any_attending_character = {
						NOT = { this = scope:host }
						is_alive = yes
						is_ai = yes
						exists = this
					}
				}
			}
			scope:activity = {
				random_attending_character = {
					limit = {
						is_alive = yes
						is_ai = yes
						NOT = { this = scope:host }
					}
					save_scope_as = dockyard_guest
				}
			}
		}
		create_character = {
			template = tournament_locale_character
			dynasty = none
			age = 70
			location = scope:activity.activity_location
			culture = scope:activity.activity_location.culture
			faith = scope:activity.activity_location.faith
			gender_female_chance = 0
			save_scope_as = dockyard_beggar
		}
	}
	option = { # Sure, teach me a thing or two
		name = agot_sailing.1015.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_trait_xp = {
					trait = lifestyle_mariner
					value >= 100
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				random_list = {
					25 = {
						#Mariner skill
						if = {
							limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
							scope:activity = {
								add_activity_log_entry = {
									key = agot_sailing_completed_log
									tags = { good }
									# this line below adds the entry to the Effects section of the conclusion UI
									show_in_conclusion = yes
									character = root

									root = {
										if = {
											limit = {
												has_trait_xp = {
													trait = lifestyle_mariner
													value < 100
												}
											}
											xp_modifiers = yes
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
						else = {
							scope:activity = {
								add_activity_log_entry = {
									key = agot_sailing_completed_log
									tags = { good }
									# this line below adds the entry to the Effects section of the conclusion UI
									show_in_conclusion = yes
									character = root

									root = { add_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
								}
							}
						}
						send_interface_toast = {
							left_icon = root
							title = agot_sailing.1014.a.success.toast
						}
					}
					75 = {
						send_interface_toast = {
							left_icon = root
							title = agot_sailing.1014.a.failure.toast
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		remove_short_term_gold = minor_gold_value
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				has_trait = greedy
				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				short_term_gold < minor_gold_value
				add = -20
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Bah! Begone!
		name = agot_sailing.1015.b
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		stress_impact = {
			compassionate = medium_stress_impact_gain
			arrogant = medium_stress_impact_loss
			just = minor_stress_impact_loss
			callous = minor_stress_impact_loss
			generous = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				has_trait = compassionate
				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = generous
				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = arrogant
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = just
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = callous
				add = 20
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # He has nothing to teach me
		name = agot_sailing.1015.c
		add_piety = minor_piety_gain
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
		}
	}

	after = {
		scope:dockyard_beggar = { silent_disappearance_effect = yes }
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.1990
			days = 15
		}
	}
}

#################
## SETTING OUT ##
#################

#Set out to Sea
agot_sailing.1990 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.1990.t
	desc = agot_sailing.1990.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = { reference = docks }
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = chancellor
	}

	trigger = {
		has_available_guest = yes
	}

	widget = {
		is_shown = { is_ai = no }
		gui = "agot_character_selection_two_options"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	immediate = {
		scope:activity = {
			every_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					is_alive = yes
					is_ai = yes
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				root = {
					add_to_variable_list = {
						name = possible_characters
						target = prev
					}
				}
			}
		}
		# Order List
		ordered_in_list = {
			variable = possible_characters
			order_by = age
			root = {
				set_variable = {
					name = si_selected_character
					value = prev
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = first_mate
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.1990.a
		var:si_selected_character = { save_scope_as = first_mate }
		clear_variable_list = possible_characters
		scope:first_mate ?= { add_prestige = 250 }
		reverse_add_opinion = {
			target = scope:first_mate
			modifier = respect_opinion
			opinion = low_positive_opinion
		}
	}

	after = {
		scope:activity = {
			every_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				trigger_event = agot_sailing.1991
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:activity = {
					has_activity_option = {
						category = special_type
						option = agot_sailing_type_hunt
					}
				}
			}
			add_character_flag = {
				flag = sailing_hunt_a_creature_misson
				years = 2
			}
			trigger_event = {
				on_action = sailing_at_sea_events
				days = 15
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:activity = {
					has_activity_option = {
						category = special_type
						option = agot_sailing_type_raid
					}
				}
			}
			add_character_flag = {
				flag = sailing_piracy_mission
				years = 2
			}
			trigger_event = {
				on_action = sailing_at_sea_events
				days = 15
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:activity = {
					has_activity_option = {
						category = special_type
						option = agot_sailing_type_valyria
					}
				}
			}
			add_character_flag = {
				flag = sailing_exploration_mission
				years = 2
			}
			trigger_event = {
				on_action = sailing_at_sea_events
				days = 15
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:activity = {
					has_activity_option = {
						category = special_type
						option = agot_sailing_type_treasure
					}
				}
			}
			add_character_flag = {
				flag = sailing_treasure_mission
				years = 2
			}
			trigger_event = {
				on_action = sailing_at_sea_events
				days = 15
			}
		}
	}
}

		reference = ocean
	}

	}

		scope:activity = {
			random_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					is_alive = yes
					is_ai = yes
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				save_scope_as = gambling_sailor_1
			}
		}
	}

		name = agot_sailing.2001.a
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain

			}
			1 = {

					has_trait = lifestyle_reveler
					add = 3
				}
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			greedy = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				add = 40
				has_trait = greedy
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # A chance to learn my crew
		trigger = {
			has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent
			has_trait_xp = {
				trait = lifestyle_mariner
				value < 100
			}
		}
		name = agot_sailing.2001.c
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
			add_trait_xp = {
				trait = lifestyle_mariner
				value = 5
			}
		}
		else = {
			random = {
				chance = 75
				add_trait = lifestyle_mariner
			}
		}
		reverse_add_opinion = {
			target = scope:gambling_sailor_1
			modifier = respect_opinion
			opinion = low_positive_opinion
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				add = 40
				has_trait = greedy
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # You won't catch me gambling!
		name = agot_sailing.2001.b
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
		stress_impact = {
			greedy = medium_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				has_trait = greedy
				add = -20
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.2099
			days = 5
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.2002 = { # Ship in Danger
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.2002.t
	desc = agot_sailing.2002.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = personality_bold
	}

	# Stop and try to help them!
	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.2002.a

		duel = {
			skill = stewardship
			value = 10
			# You succeed in saving the crew and some of their haul!
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.2002.a.tt_success
				send_interface_toast = {
					title = agot_sailing.2002.a.tt_success
					left_icon = root
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent
							OR = {
								NOT = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
								AND = {
									has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
									has_trait_xp = {
										trait = lifestyle_mariner
										value < 100
									}
								}
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
							add_trait_xp = {
								trait = lifestyle_mariner
								value = 5
							}
						}
						else = { add_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
					}
					else = { add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain }
				}
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.2020
					}
				}
			}
			# You fail, and many are lost to the storm.
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.2002.a.tt_failure
				send_interface_toast = {
					title = agot_sailing.2002.a.tt_failure
					left_icon = root
					add_prestige = miniscule_prestige_loss
				}
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.2021
					}
				}
			}
		}

		stress_impact = {
			diligent = minor_stress_impact_loss
			compassionate = medium_stress_impact_loss
			lazy = minor_stress_impact_gain
			callous = medium_stress_impact_gain
			sadistic = medium_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			ai_value_modifier = {
				ai_compassion = 0.75
				ai_energy = 0.25
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = 10
				has_trait = diligent
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = 20
				has_trait = compassionate
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight down for stress.
				add = -10
				has_trait = lazy
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight down for stress.
				add = -20
				has_trait = callous
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight down for stress.
				add = -20
				has_trait = sadistic
			}
		}
	}

	# Salvage the cargo, kill the survivors.
	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.2002.b

		# Prowess duel to storm the boat.
		duel = {
			skill = prowess
			value = 10
			# You storm the boat easily.
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.2002.b.tt_success
				send_interface_toast = {
					title = agot_sailing.2002.b.tt_success
					left_icon = root
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent
							OR = {
								NOT = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
								AND = {
									has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
									has_trait_xp = {
										trait = lifestyle_mariner
										value < 100
									}
								}
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
							add_trait_xp = {
								trait = lifestyle_mariner
								value = 5
							}
						}
						else = { add_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
					}
					else = { add_gold = minor_gold_value }
				}
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.2022
					}
				}
			}
			# Survivors slip to shore.
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.2002.b.tt_failure
				send_interface_toast = {
					title = agot_sailing.2002.b.tt_failure
					left_icon = root
					add_gold = minor_gold_value
					add_dread = minor_dread_gain
					add_tyranny = minor_tyranny_gain
					increase_wounds_effect = { REASON = fight }
				}
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.2023

				}

		}

		}

				ai_boldness = 0.5
				ai_greed = 0.25

				ai_honor = -0.5
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = 10

			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = 10
				has_trait = callous
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = 20
				has_trait = sadistic
			}

agot_sailing.2004 = { # Pirates!
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.2004.t
	desc = agot_sailing.2004.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = personality_bold
	}

	# Let them come!
	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.2004.a
		duel = {
			skill = prowess
			value = high_skill_rating
			# You fight off the pirates
			60 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				min = 5
				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.2004.a.success
				send_interface_toast = {
					title = agot_sailing.2004.a.success
					left_icon = root
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								has_trait_xp = {
									trait = lifestyle_mariner
									value >= 100
								}
							}
						}
						# Mariner skill
						if = {
							limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
							scope:activity = {
								add_activity_log_entry = {
									key = agot_sailing_completed_log
									tags = { good }
									# this line below adds the entry to the Effects section of the conclusion UI
									show_in_conclusion = yes
									character = root

									root = {
										if = {
											limit = {
												has_trait_xp = {
													trait = lifestyle_mariner
													value < 100
												}
											}
											xp_modifiers = yes
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
						else = {
							scope:activity = {
								add_activity_log_entry = {
									key = agot_sailing_completed_log
									tags = { good }
									# this line below adds the entry to the Effects section of the conclusion UI
									show_in_conclusion = yes
									character = root

									root = { add_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
								}
							}
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent }
						}
						add_short_term_gold = minor_gold_value
						add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
					}
				}
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.2031
					}
				}
			}
			# They plunder your ship
			40 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				min = 10
				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.2004.a.failure
				scope:host = {
					send_interface_toast = {
						title = agot_sailing.2004.a.failure
						left_icon = root
						remove_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value
						add_stress = 25
					}
				}
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.2032
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				has_trait = brave
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = craven
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = greedy
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				prowess >= 15
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				prowess < 15
				add = -10
			}
		}
	}
	# Hoist the sails!
	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.2004.b
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_loss
		add_stress = -10
		scope:activity = {
			every_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				trigger_event = agot_sailing.2033
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				has_trait = brave
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = craven
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = greedy
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				prowess >= 15
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				prowess < 15
				add = 10
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.2099
			days = 5
		}
	}
}

				has_trait = compassionate

				has_trait = generous
			}
		}

			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
			just = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			ai_value_modifier = {
				ai_compassion = -0.25
				ai_energy = -0.5
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = 10
				has_trait = lazy
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = 10
				has_trait = callous
			}

agot_sailing.2006 = { # A Game of Cards
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.2006.t
	desc = agot_sailing.2006.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	override_background = {
		reference = tavern
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = personality_rational
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:first_mate
		animation = steward
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:first_mate = { is_alive = no }
			}
			set_first_mate = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Playing straight
		name = agot_sailing.2006.a
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				desc = agot_sailing.2006.a.tt_success
				send_interface_toast = {
					title = agot_sailing.2006.a.tt_success
					left_icon = root
					add_gold = activity_minor_gold_value
				}
			}
			50 = {
				desc = agot_sailing.2006.a.tt_failure
				send_interface_toast = {
					title = agot_sailing.2006.a.tt_failure
					left_icon = root
					remove_short_term_gold = activity_minor_gold_value
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				ai_honor >= 15
				add = 50
			}
			modifier = {
				ai_honor < -15
				add = -50
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Cheating
		name = agot_sailing.2006.b
		#Intrigue duel to win at cards
		stress_impact = {
			honest = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		duel = {
			skill = intrigue
			value = 10
			# You use the aces up your sleeves to win the game
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.2006.b.tt_success
				send_interface_toast = {
					title = agot_sailing.2006.b.tt_success
					left_icon = root
					add_gold = activity_minor_gold_value
				}
			}

			# You get caught by the sailors
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.2006.b.tt_failure
				send_interface_toast = {
					title = agot_sailing.2006.b.tt_failure
					left_icon = root
					remove_short_term_gold = activity_minor_gold_value
					add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
					add_stress = minor_stress_impact_gain
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				ai_honor < 50
				add = 50
			}
			modifier = {
				ai_honor >= 50
				add = -50
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = honest
				add = -20
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Staying out
		name = agot_sailing.2006.c
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		stress_impact = {
			greedy = minor_stress_impact_gain
			content = minor_stress_impact_loss
			gregarious = medium_stress_impact_loss
			drunkard = major_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				has_trait = greedy
				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = content
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = gregarious
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = drunkard
				add = 20
			}
		}
	}

			id = agot_sailing.2099
			days = 5
		}

			skill = diplomacy
			value = 10
			# The crew rises above the whinging
			50 = {

					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.2007.a.tt_success
				send_interface_toast = {
					title = agot_sailing.2007.a.tt_success
					left_icon = root

						limit = {
							has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent
							OR = {
								NOT = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }

									has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
									has_trait_xp = {
										trait = lifestyle_mariner
										value < 100
									}
								}
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
							add_trait_xp = {
								trait = lifestyle_mariner
								value = 5
							}
						}
						else = { add_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
					}
					else = {
						add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
					}
				}
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.2040
					}
				}
			}
			# Essos when?

					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.2007.a.tt_failure
				send_interface_toast = {
					title = agot_sailing.2007.a.tt_failure
					left_icon = root
					add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
					add_stress = minor_stress_impact_gain
				}
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.2041
					}
				}
			}
		}

		stress_impact = {
			greedy = minor_stress_impact_loss
			callous = minor_stress_impact_loss
			sadistic = medium_stress_impact_loss
			compassionate = medium_stress_impact_gain
			generous = medium_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}

agot_sailing.2009 = { # The Stars Shine the Same
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.2009.t
	desc = agot_sailing.2009.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = personality_honorable
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:stargazer
		animation = personality_rational
	}

	trigger = {
		has_available_guest = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		scope:activity = {
			random_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					is_alive = yes
					is_ai = yes
					NOT = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				alternative_limit = {
					is_alive = yes
					is_ai = yes
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				save_scope_as = stargazer
			}
		}
	}

	# Navigation
	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.2009.a
		trigger = {
			scope:stargazer = {
				NOT = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
			}
			OR = {
				has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
				learning > 10
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
				}
			}
			random = {
				chance = 33
				send_interface_toast = {
					left_icon = root
					title = agot_sailing.2009.a.success.toast
					scope:stargazer = { add_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
					# Mariner skill
					custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.2009.a.mariner_progress
					if = {
						limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
						scope:activity = {
							add_activity_log_entry = {
								key = agot_sailing_completed_log
								tags = { good }
								# this line below adds the entry to the Effects section of the conclusion UI
								show_in_conclusion = yes
								character = root

								root = {
									if = {
										limit = {
											has_trait_xp = {
												trait = lifestyle_mariner
												value < 100
											}
										}
										xp_modifiers = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					else = {
						scope:activity = {
							add_activity_log_entry = {
								key = agot_sailing_completed_log
								tags = { good }
								# this line below adds the entry to the Effects section of the conclusion UI
								show_in_conclusion = yes
								character = root

								root = { add_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			random = {
				chance = 33
				add_learning_skill = 1
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
		}
	}

	# Religious
	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.2009.b
		stress_impact = {
			cynical = medium_stress_impact_gain
		}
		add_piety = 50
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				has_trait = cynical
				add = -20
			}
		}
	}

	# Place in the world
	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.2009.c
		stress_impact = {
			base = 40
			ambitious = medium_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				has_trait = ambitious
				add = -20
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.2099
			days = 5
		}
	}
}

				has_trait = compassionate
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = brave
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = craven
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = callous
				add = 10
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.2099
			days = 5
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.2011 = { # Sudden Fog
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.2011.t
	desc = agot_sailing.2011.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	override_effect_2d = {
		reference = fog
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = worry
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:first_mate
		animation = survey
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:first_mate = { is_alive = no }
			}
			set_first_mate = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Keep your heading
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
		}
		name = agot_sailing.2011.a
		stress_impact = {
			craven = medium_stress_impact_gain
			brave = minor_stress_impact_loss
			impatient = medium_stress_impact_loss
			patient = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		duel = {
			skill = stewardship
			value = high_skill_rating
			60 = { # Find a swift path through the fog
				desc = agot_sailing.2011.a.success
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 2
				}
				send_interface_toast = {
					title = agot_sailing.2011.a.success
					left_icon = root
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent
						}
						add_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					}
					else = {
						add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
						add_stress = -25
					}
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_sailing.2099
					days = 5
				}
				# Mariner skill
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = lifestyle_mariner
								value >= 100
							}
						}
					}
					scope:activity = {
						add_activity_log_entry = {
							key = agot_sailing_completed_log
							tags = { good }
							# this line below adds the entry to the Effects section of the conclusion UI
							show_in_conclusion = yes
							character = root

							root = {
								xp_modifiers = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.2049
					}
				}
			}
			40 = { # Get lost in the fog
				desc = agot_sailing.2011.a.failure
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -2
				}
				send_interface_toast = {
					title = agot_sailing.2011.a.failure
					left_icon = root
					add_stress = minor_stress_impact_gain
					add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_sailing.2099
					days = 25
				}
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.2050
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				has_trait = craven
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = brave
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = patient
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = impatient
				add = 10
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #I'm a mariner, I know what to do
		name = agot_sailing.2011.c
		trigger = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
		show_as_unavailable = {
			always = yes
		}
		stress_impact = {
			craven = medium_stress_impact_gain
			brave = minor_stress_impact_loss
			impatient = medium_stress_impact_loss
			patient = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.2099
			days = 5
		}
		scope:activity = {
			every_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				trigger_event = agot_sailing.2052
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
		}
	}

	option = { #Turn the ship
		name = agot_sailing.2011.b
		stress_impact = {
			brave = medium_stress_impact_gain
			impatient = medium_stress_impact_gain
			craven = minor_stress_impact_loss
			patient = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
		scope:activity = {
			every_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				trigger_event = agot_sailing.2051
			}
		}
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.2099
			days = 20
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				has_trait = craven
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = brave
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = patient
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = impatient
				add = -10
			}
		}
	}

agot_sailing.2020 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.2020.t
	desc = {
		desc = agot_sailing.2020.desc
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					OR = {
						has_trait = callous
						has_trait = sadistic
					}
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.2020.desc.jerk
			}
			desc = agot_sailing.2020.desc.normal
		}
	}
	theme = sailing_activity
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = callous
					has_trait = sadistic
				}
			}
			animation = personality_callous
		}
		animation = admiration
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:host
		animation = personality_bold
	}
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}

	option = { # Good work
		name = agot_sailing.2020.a

		stress_impact = {
			diligent = minor_stress_impact_loss
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_loss
			callous = minor_stress_impact_gain
			sadistic = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			ai_value_modifier = {
				ai_compassion = 0.75
				ai_energy = 0.25
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = 10
				has_trait = diligent
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = 20
				has_trait = compassionate
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight down for stress.
				add = -20
				has_trait = callous
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight down for stress.
				add = -20
				has_trait = sadistic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # What was he thinking...
		name = agot_sailing.2020.b
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_trait = callous
				has_trait = sadistic
			}
		}
		add_opinion = {
			modifier = disappointed_opinion
			target = scope:host
			opinion = -10
		}

		stress_impact = {
			callous = minor_stress_impact_loss
			sadistic = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			ai_value_modifier = {
				ai_compassion = -0.75
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = 20
				has_trait = callous
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = 20
				has_trait = sadistic
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		trigger = { has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent }
		name = agot_sailing.2020.c
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}
				add_trait_xp = {
					trait = lifestyle_mariner
					value = 5
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			random = {
				chance = 75
				add_trait = lifestyle_mariner
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 150
		}
	}
}

		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = {
				has_trait = zealous
			}
			animation = worry
		}
		animation = celebrate_spear
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:host
		animation = celebrate_spear
	}

	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.2028.a

		add_opinion = {
			modifier = impressed_opinion
			target = scope:host
			opinion = 40
		}

		stress_impact = {
			lifestyle_mariner = minor_stress_impact_loss
			lifestyle_hunter = minor_stress_impact_loss
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			ai_value_modifier = {
				ai_energy = -0.25
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = -40
				has_trait = compassionate
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = 20
				has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = 20
				has_trait = lifestyle_hunter
			}
		}
	}

	option = {

			opinion = -20
		}

		stress_impact = {

			}

				has_trait = compassionate

#####################################
#	INTENT EVENTS					#
#	2099 - 2999						#
#####################################

agot_sailing.2099 = { # Get the appropriate intent event
	hidden = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:host = { has_activity_intent = reduce_stress_intent }
			}
			trigger_event = agot_sailing.2100
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:host = { has_activity_intent = woo_attendee_intent }
			}
			trigger_event = agot_sailing.2101
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:host = { has_activity_intent = murder_attendee_intent }
			}
			trigger_event = agot_sailing.2201
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:host = { has_activity_intent = befriend_attendee_intent }
			}
			trigger_event = agot_sailing.2301
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:host = { has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent }
			}
			trigger_event = agot_sailing.2399
		}
		else = {
			trigger_event = {
				id = agot_sailing.9002
				days = 1
			}
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.2100 = { # Stress Reduction
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.2100.t
	theme = sailing_activity
	desc = agot_sailing.2100.desc
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = happiness
	}
	option = { #A Rush Across the Ocean
		name = agot_sailing.2100.a
		add_stress = -50
	}
	after = { # Get the correct destination
		trigger_event = agot_sailing.2399
	}
}

agot_sailing.2101 = { # Seduction
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.2101.t
	theme = stewardship
	desc = {
		desc = agot_sailing.2101.opening
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				exists = scope:woo_target.primary_spouse
				NOT = { is_consort_of = scope:woo_target }
				scope:woo_target.primary_spouse = { is_participant_in_activity = scope:activity }
			}
			desc = agot_sailing.2101.married
		}
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				exists = scope:woo_target.primary_spouse
				NOT = { is_consort_of = scope:woo_target }
				NOT = { scope:woo_target.primary_spouse = { is_participant_in_activity = scope:activity } }
			}
			desc = agot_sailing.2101.married_absent
		}
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				exists = scope:woo_target.primary_spouse
				is_consort_of = scope:woo_target
			}
			desc = agot_sailing.2101.currentspouse
		}
		desc = agot_sailing.2101.desc
	}

	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = flirtation
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:woo_target
		animation = happiness
	}

	cooldown = { years = 1 }

	trigger = {
		exists = intent_target
		intent_target = {
			is_alive = yes
			is_participant_in_activity = scope:activity
			NOT = { this = root }
		}
	}

	on_trigger_fail = { # Get the correct destination
		trigger_event = agot_sailing.2399
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 10
			has_activity_intent = woo_attendee_intent
			exists = intent_target
			intent_target = {
				is_alive = yes
				is_participant_in_activity = scope:activity
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = woo_actor
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = intent_target
				intent_target = {
					is_alive = yes
					is_participant_in_activity = scope:activity
					NOT = { this = root }
				}
			}
			intent_target = {
				save_scope_as = woo_target
				save_scope_as = target
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #A Rush Across the Ocean
		name = agot_sailing.2101.a
		trigger = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.2101.a.tt
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = woo_approach
			value = flag:sailor
		}
		stress_impact = {
			craven = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			ai_value_modifier = {
				ai_energy = 0.5
				ai_boldness = 0.5
				ai_compassion = -0.5
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = -20
				has_trait = craven
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Master Mariner
		name = agot_sailing.2101.b
		trigger = {
			has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
			has_trait_xp = {
				trait = lifestyle_mariner
				value >= trait_second_level
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.2101.b.tt
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = woo_approach
			value = flag:socialite
		}
		stress_impact = {
			shy = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = -20
				has_trait = shy
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Pity
		name = agot_sailing.2101.c
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.2101.c.tt
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = woo_approach
			value = flag:pity
		}
		stress_impact = {
			sadistic = minor_stress_impact_gain
			callous = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			opinion_modifier = {
				opinion_target = scope:woo_target
				multiplier = 0.5
			}
			ai_value_modifier = {
				ai_energy = -0.5
				ai_boldness = -0.5
				ai_compassion = 0.5
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = -20
				has_trait = sadistic
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = -20
				has_trait = callous
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Wastrel
		name = agot_sailing.2101.d
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.2101.d.tt
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = woo_approach
			value = flag:wastrel
		}
		stress_impact = {
			lifestyle_mariner = minor_stress_impact_gain
			content = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			ai_value_modifier = {
				ai_boldness = 1
				ai_compassion = 0.5
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = -20
				has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = -20
				has_trait = content
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Steward
		name = agot_sailing.2101.e
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.2101.e.tt
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = woo_approach
			value = flag:steward
		}
		stress_impact = {
			impatient = minor_stress_impact_gain
			lazy = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			ai_value_modifier = {
				ai_boldness = 1
				ai_compassion = 0.5
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = -20
				has_trait = lazy
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = -20
				has_trait = impatient
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		show_as_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					desc = seduce_ongoing_possible_outcomes_tooltip_effect.success.desc
					show_chance = no
					scope:woo_target = {
						progress_towards_lover_effect = {
							CHARACTER = root
							OPINION = 15
							REASON = intent_sailing_conversation
						}
					}
				}
				10 = {
					desc = seduce_ongoing_possible_outcomes_tooltip_effect.failure.desc
					show_chance = no
					reverse_add_opinion = {
						target = scope:woo_target
						modifier = hunt_failed_woo_opinion
						opinion = -10
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:woo_target = {
			save_scope_as = target
			hidden_effect = {
				#Evaluate what makes for a "right" choice
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							AND = {
								OR = {
									has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
									ai_energy > medium_positive_energy
								}
								exists = scope:woo_approach
								scope:woo_approach = flag:sailor
							}
							AND = {
								OR = {
									AND = {
										has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
										has_trait_xp = {
											trait = lifestyle_mariner
											value >= trait_second_level
										}
									}
									ai_sociability > medium_positive_compassion # socialbility doesn't exist
								}
								exists = scope:woo_approach
								scope:woo_approach = flag:socialite
							}
							AND = {
								OR = {
									has_personality_benevolent_trigger = yes
									ai_compassion > medium_positive_compassion
								}
								exists = scope:woo_approach
								scope:woo_approach = flag:pity
							}
							AND = {
								OR = {
									has_trait = diligent
									ai_boldness > medium_positive_boldness
								}
								exists = scope:woo_approach
								scope:woo_approach = flag:wastrel
							}
							AND = {
								OR = {
									has_trait = education_stewardship
									ai_rationality > medium_positive_rationality
								}
								exists = scope:woo_approach
								scope:woo_approach = flag:steward
							}
						}
					}
					save_scope_value_as = {
						name = made_great_choice
						value = yes
					}
				}
				random_list = {
					100 = { #Success
						modifier = {
							add = 100
							exists = scope:woo_approach
							scope:woo_approach = flag:sailor
							has_trait = brave
						}
						modifier = {
							add = 100
							exists = scope:woo_approach
							scope:woo_approach = flag:socialite
							has_trait = gregarious
						}
						modifier = {
							add = 100
							exists = scope:woo_approach
							scope:woo_approach = flag:pity
							has_trait = compassionate
						}
						modifier = {
							add = 100
							exists = scope:woo_approach
							scope:woo_approach = flag:wastrel
							has_trait = diligent
						}
						modifier = {
							add = 100
							exists = scope:woo_approach
							scope:woo_approach = flag:steward
							has_trait = architect
						}
						trigger_event = {
							id = agot_sailing.2102
							days = { 1 2 }
						}
						root = {
							trigger_event = {
								id = agot_sailing.2104
								days = { 3 4 }
							}
						}
					}
					100 = { #Failure
						trigger = {
							NOT = { exists = scope:made_great_choice }
						}
						modifier = {
							add = 100
							exists = scope:woo_approach
							OR = {
								scope:woo_approach = flag:sailor
								scope:woo_approach = flag:socialite
							}
						}
						modifier = {
							add = 100
							exists = scope:woo_approach
							scope:woo_approach = flag:sailor
							has_trait = craven
						}
						modifier = {
							add = 100
							exists = scope:woo_approach
							scope:woo_approach = flag:socialite
							has_trait = shy
						}
						modifier = {
							add = 100
							exists = scope:woo_approach
							scope:woo_approach = flag:pity
							OR = {
								has_trait = callous
								has_trait = sadistic
							}
						}
						modifier = {
							add = 100
							exists = scope:woo_approach
							scope:woo_approach = flag:wastrel
							has_trait = content
						}
						modifier = {
							add = 100
							exists = scope:woo_approach
							scope:woo_approach = flag:steward
							has_trait = lazy
						}
						trigger_event = {
							id = agot_sailing.2103
							days = { 1 2 }
						}
						root = {
							trigger_event = {
								id = agot_sailing.2105
								days = { 3 4 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			}

			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { scope:woo_approach = flag:wastrel }
				desc = agot_sailing.2102.wastrel
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { scope:woo_approach = flag:steward }
				desc = agot_sailing.2102.steward
			}
			desc = agot_sailing.2102.fallback
