## Murder
agot_sailing.2201 = { # Murder: final prep
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.2201.t
	desc = agot_sailing.2201.desc
	theme = intrigue
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = scheme
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:murder_target
		animation = throne_room_conversation_2
		camera = camera_event_left
	}

	trigger = {
		exists = intent_target
		intent_target = {
			is_alive = yes
			is_participant_in_activity = scope:activity
			NOT = { this = root }
		}
	}

	on_trigger_fail = { # Get the correct destination
		trigger_event = agot_sailing.2399
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = intent_target
				intent_target = {
					is_alive = yes
					is_participant_in_activity = scope:activity
					NOT = { this = root }
				}
			}
			intent_target = {
				save_scope_as = murder_target
				save_scope_as = target
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Today is the day
		name = agot_sailing.2201.a
		custom_tooltip = sailing_murder_accomplice_tt
		stress_impact = {
			patient = minor_stress_impact_gain
			craven = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		trigger_event = agot_sailing.2202
	}
}

agot_sailing.2202 = { # Murder: do the deed
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.2201.t
	desc = {
		desc = agot_sailing.2202.opening
		desc = agot_sailing.2202.desc
	}
	theme = intrigue
	override_background = {
		reference = fp1_tribal_corridor
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = scheme
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:murder_target
		animation = thinking
		camera = camera_event_left
	}

	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = murder_actor
	}

	option = { #Take the shot!
		name = agot_sailing.2202.a
		flavor = agot_sailing.2202.a.tt
		reason = activity_intent

		duel = {
			skills = { intrigue prowess }
			target = scope:murder_target
			10 = { # ...but target is killed
				desc = sailing_kill_murder_killed
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 0.5
				}
				custom_tooltip = sailing_target_attendee_killed_tt
				save_scope_as = murder_success
				min = 2
			}
			10 = { # ...the target survives
				desc = sailing_kill_murder_escape
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -0.5
				}
				custom_tooltip = sailing_target_attendee_injured_chance_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					random = {
						chance = 50
						save_scope_as = murder_injury
					}
				}
				min = 2
			}
		}
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.2203
			days = 1
		}
		stress_impact = {
			vengeful = medium_stress_impact_loss
			sadistic = medium_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			ai_value_modifier = {
				ai_vengefulness = 0.5
				ai_energy = 0.5
				ai_boldness = 0.25
				ai_honor = -0.25
				ai_compassion = -0.25
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Restrain yourself
		name = agot_sailing.2202.b
		flavor = agot_sailing.2202.b.tt
		scope:activity = {
			add_activity_log_entry = {
				key = sailing_murder_restrain_key
				score = 25
				tags = { random intent }
				character = root
				target = scope:murder_target

				# Effect
				root = {
					stress_impact = {
						base = minor_stress_gain
						sadistic = minor_stress_impact_gain
						wrathful = minor_stress_impact_gain
						vengeful = minor_stress_impact_gain
						impatient = minor_stress_impact_gain
						compassionate = medium_stress_impact_loss
						forgiving = medium_stress_impact_loss
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			ai_value_modifier = {
				ai_honor = 0.75
				ai_rationality = 0.25
				ai_compassion = 0.25
				ai_vengefulness = -0.25
			}
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.2203 = { # Murder: Outcome
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.2201.t
	desc = {
		desc = agot_sailing.2203.desc
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { exists = scope:murder_success }
				desc = agot_sailing.2203.success
			}
			desc = agot_sailing.2203.failure
		}
	}
	theme = intrigue
	override_background = {
		reference = fp1_tribal_corridor
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { exists = scope:murder_success }
			animation = schadenfreude
		}
		animation = stress
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:murder_target
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { exists = scope:murder_success }
			animation = loss_1
		}
		animation = fear
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_scheme"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:murder_success }
			scope:activity = {
				add_activity_log_entry = {
					key = sailing_kill_murder_killed
					score = 25
					tags = { random intent }
					character = root
					target = scope:murder_target

					# Effect
					show_as_tooltip = {
						unknown_murder_effect = { VICTIM = scope:murder_target MURDERER = root REASON = death_drowned }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			scope:activity = {
				add_activity_log_entry = {
					key = sailing_kill_murder_missed
					score = 25
					tags = { random intent }
					character = root
					target = scope:murder_target

					# Effect
					root = {
						show_as_tooltip = {
							if = {
								limit = { exists = scope:murder_injury }
								scope:murder_target = {
									increase_wounds_effect = { REASON = assassination }
								}
							}
							attempted_murder_opinion_effect = {
								VICTIM = scope:murder_target
								MURDERER = root
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:murder_target = { trigger_event = agot_sailing.2204 }
	}

	option = { # Success
		name = agot_sailing.2203.a
		trigger = { exists = scope:murder_success }
		trigger_event = agot_sailing.2399
	}

	option = { # Failure
		name = agot_sailing.2203.b
		trigger = {
			NOT = { exists = scope:murder_success }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { this = scope:host }
			custom_tooltip = sailing_ends_tt
			invalidate_activity = scope:activity
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.2204 = { # Murder: Target
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.2204.t
	desc = {
		desc = agot_sailing.2204.desc
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { exists = scope:murder_success }
				desc = agot_sailing.2204.success
			}
			desc = agot_sailing.2204.failure
		}
	}
	theme = hunt_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = fp1_tribal_corridor
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { exists = scope:murder_success }
			animation = loss_1
		}
		animation = fear
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:murder_actor
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { exists = scope:murder_success }
			animation = schadenfreude
		}
		animation = stress
	}
	cooldown = { years = 1 }

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_scheme"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:murder_success }
			unknown_murder_effect = { VICTIM = scope:murder_target MURDERER = scope:murder_actor REASON = death_drowned }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						scope:murder_actor = scope:host
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = invalidated_flavor
				scope:host = { trigger_event = agot_sailing.9002 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = scope:murder_injury }
				increase_wounds_effect = { REASON = assassination }
			}
			attempted_murder_opinion_effect = {
				VICTIM = scope:murder_target
				MURDERER = scope:murder_actor
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Success
		name = agot_sailing.2204.a
		trigger = { exists = scope:murder_success }
	}

	option = { # Failure
		name = agot_sailing.2204.b
		trigger = {
			NOT = { exists = scope:murder_success }
		}
		custom_tooltip = sailing_ends_tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					scope:murder_actor = scope:host
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = invalidated_flavor
			scope:murder_actor = { save_scope_as = invalidated_culprit }
			scope:host = { trigger_event = agot_sailing.9002 }
		}
	}
}

## Befriend
agot_sailing.2301 = { # Befriend Attempt
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.2301.t
	desc = agot_sailing.2301.desc
	theme = hunt_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = happiness
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:befriend_target
	}
	cooldown = { years = 1 }

	trigger = {
		exists = intent_target
		intent_target = {
			is_alive = yes
			is_participant_in_activity = scope:activity
			NOT = { this = root }
		}
	}

	on_trigger_fail = { # Get the correct destination
		trigger_event = agot_sailing.2399
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 10
			has_activity_intent = befriend_attendee_intent
			exists = intent_target
			intent_target = {
				is_alive = yes
				is_participant_in_activity = scope:activity
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		root = { save_scope_as = befriend_actor }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_activity_intent = befriend_attendee_intent
				exists = intent_target
				intent_target = {
					is_alive = yes
					is_participant_in_activity = scope:activity
					NOT = { this = root }
				}
			}
			intent_target = { save_scope_as = befriend_target }
		}
		else = {
			random_scheme = {
				limit = {
					scheme_type = befriend
					scheme_target_character = {
						is_alive = yes
						is_participant_in_activity = scope:activity
						NOT = { this = root }
					}
				}
				scheme_target_character = { save_scope_as = befriend_target }
			}
		}
		scope:befriend_target = {
			save_scope_as = woo_target
			save_scope_as = target
		}
	}

	option = { # Fellow Sailor
		name = agot_sailing.2101.b
		trigger = {
			has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
			scope:befriend_target = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
		}
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.2101.b.tt
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = befriend_approach
			value = flag:sailor
		}
		stress_impact = {
			shy = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = -20
				has_trait = shy
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # pity
		name = agot_sailing.2101.c
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.2101.c.tt
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = befriend_approach
			value = flag:pity
		}
		stress_impact = {
			sadistic = minor_stress_impact_gain
			callous = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			opinion_modifier = {
				opinion_target = scope:first_mate
				multiplier = 0.5
			}
			ai_value_modifier = {
				ai_energy = -0.5
				ai_boldness = -0.5
				ai_compassion = 0.5
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = -20
				has_trait = sadistic
			}
			modifier = {	# Weight up for stress.
				add = -20
				has_trait = callous
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Wastrel
		name = agot_sailing.2101.d
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.2101.d.tt
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = befriend_approach
			value = flag:wastrel
		}
		stress_impact = {
			lifestyle_mariner = minor_stress_impact_gain
			content = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			ai_value_modifier = {
				ai_boldness = 1
				ai_compassion = 0.5
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Steward
		name = agot_sailing.2101.e
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.2101.e.tt
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = befriend_approach
			value = flag:steward
		}
		stress_impact = {
			impatient = minor_stress_impact_gain
			lazy = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			ai_value_modifier = {
				ai_boldness = 1
				ai_compassion = 0.5
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		show_as_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					desc = seduce_ongoing_possible_outcomes_tooltip_effect.success.desc
					show_chance = no
					scope:befriend_target = {
						progress_towards_friend_effect = {
							CHARACTER = root
							OPINION = 15
							REASON = friend_hunt_conversation_impressed
						}
					}
				}
				10 = {
					desc = seduce_ongoing_possible_outcomes_tooltip_effect.failure.desc
					show_chance = no
					reverse_add_opinion = {
						target = scope:befriend_target
						modifier = hunt_bored_opinion
						opinion = -10
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:befriend_target = {
			save_scope_as = target
			hidden_effect = {
				#Evaluate what makes for a "right" choice
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							AND = {
								ai_energy > medium_positive_energy
								ai_sociability > medium_positive_compassion # sociability doesn't exist
								hunt_dharmic_pacificist_trigger = no
								exists = scope:befriend_approach
								scope:befriend_approach = flag:sailor
							}
							AND = {
								has_trait = humble
								opinion = {
									target = root
									value >= 25
								}
								exists = scope:befriend_approach
								scope:befriend_approach = flag:pity
							}
							AND = {
								OR = {
									has_trait = ambitious
									ai_boldness > medium_positive_boldness
								}
								exists = scope:befriend_approach
								scope:befriend_approach = flag:wastrel
							}
							AND = {
								OR = {
									has_trait = education_stewardship
									ai_rationality > medium_positive_rationality
								}
								exists = scope:befriend_approach
								scope:befriend_approach = flag:steward
							}
						}
					}
					save_scope_value_as = {
						name = made_great_choice
						value = yes
					}
				}
				random_list = {
					100 = { #Success
						modifier = {
							add = 100
							exists = scope:befriend_approach
							scope:befriend_approach = flag:sailor
							has_trait = gregarious
						}
						modifier = {
							add = 100
							exists = scope:befriend_approach
							scope:befriend_approach = flag:pity
							has_trait = compassionate
						}
						trigger_event = {
							id = agot_sailing.2302
							days = { 1 2 }
						}
						scope:befriend_actor = {
							trigger_event = {
								id = agot_sailing.2304
								days = { 3 4 }
							}
						}
					}
					100 = { #Failure
						trigger = {
							NOT = { exists = scope:made_great_choice }
						}
						modifier = {
							add = 100
							exists = scope:befriend_approach
							scope:befriend_approach = flag:sailor
							has_trait = craven
						}
						modifier = {
							add = 100
							exists = scope:befriend_approach
							scope:befriend_approach = flag:pity
							OR = {
								has_trait = callous
								has_trait = sadistic
							}
						}
						modifier = {
							add = 100
							exists = scope:befriend_approach
							scope:befriend_approach = flag:wastrel
							has_trait = content
						}
						modifier = {
							add = 100
							exists = scope:befriend_approach
							scope:befriend_approach = flag:steward
							has_trait = lazy
						}
						trigger_event = {
							id = agot_sailing.2303
							days = { 1 2 }
						}
						scope:befriend_actor = {
							trigger_event = {
								id = agot_sailing.2305
								days = { 3 4 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.2399 = { # Hidden: Get the correct destination
	hidden = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_exploration_mission }
			trigger_event = { # Start of Valyria chain
				id = agot_sailing.3100
				days = 10
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_piracy_mission }
			trigger_event = { # Start of Piracy chain
				id = agot_sailing.9100
				days = 10
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_hunt_a_creature_misson }
			trigger_event = { # Start of whaling chain
				id = agot_sailing.5500
				days = 10
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_treasure_mission }
			trigger_event = { # Start of treasure hunt chain
				id = agot_sailing.6001
				days = 10
			}
		}
		else = {
			trigger_event = { # Fallback: end of activity event
				id = agot_sailing.9002
				days = 5
			}
		}
	}
}

############################
## Valyria Chain
## 3000-3999
############################

## Tests
# Learning - Direction to head? - knowledge of history and navigation by the stars
# Prowess - Sea Monster
# Stewardship / Martial - Supplies running low - you've been sailing in circles, and now your supplies are starting to run low. Do you turn back, keep going, try to implement rationing, etc
# Diplomacy - Madness of the Wave - Crew gets visions of Valyria being consumed by flame, lose their nerves, potentially go bad - chance to comfort/rally them to go onward, or have to turn back because your crew broke and mutiny
# Mariner - Fury of the Deep - a terrible storm appears, try to fight it or go back

agot_sailing.3100 = { # The First Test - Learning - Charting a Course
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.3100.t
	desc = agot_sailing.3100.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = personality_rational
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:first_mate
		animation = personality_compassionate
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:first_mate = { is_alive = no }
			}
			set_first_mate = yes
		}
		scope:activity = { set_variable = too_far_gone }
	}

	option = { # Which way is Valyria? Try to figure it out!
		name = agot_sailing.3100.a
		duel = {
			skill = learning
			value = valyria_skill_challenge
			60 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					has_trait = scholar
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_valyrian }
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 20
					has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 30
					has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 50
					has_character_modifier = reached_valyria
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 25
					has_character_modifier = guest_reached_valyria
				}
				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.3100.a.success.tt
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_sailing.3101
					days = 1
				}
			}
			40 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					has_trait = impatient
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -10
					has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -20
					has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -30
					has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.3100.a.failure.tt
				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing_end_failure
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_sailing.3102
					days = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		}

	option = { # Command the Kraken
		trigger = {
			any_character_artifact = { has_variable = krakenhorn }
		}
		name = agot_sailing.3200.c

		random_list = {
			75 = {
				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.3200.c_success
				add_character_flag = used_horn
				modifier = {
					add = 15
					dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Celtigar
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_sailing.3201
					days = 1
				}
			}
			25 = {
				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.3200.c_failure
				modifier = {
					add = -15
					dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Celtigar
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					scope:activity = {
						every_attending_character = {
							limit = {
								NOT = { this = root }
							}
							death = { death_reason = death_crushed_by_kraken }
						}
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					scope:activity = {
						every_attending_character = {
							limit = {
								NOT = { this = root }
								is_ai = yes
							}
							death = { death_reason = death_crushed_by_kraken }
						}
						every_attending_character = {
							limit = {
								NOT = { this = root }
								is_ai = no
							}
							trigger_event = agot_sailing.3923 # Kill event
						}
					}
				}
				death = { death_reason = death_crushed_by_kraken }
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_equipped_character_artifact = {
					OR = {
						artifact_type = sword
						artifact_type = axe
					}
					has_variable = valyrian_steel
				}
			}
			random_equipped_character_artifact = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							artifact_type = sword
							artifact_type = axe
						}
						has_variable = valyrian_steel
					}
					save_scope_as = valyrian_steel_weapon
					scope:activity = {
						set_variable = {
							name = brought_vs_from_home
							value = scope:valyrian_steel_weapon
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_equipped_character_artifact = {
					artifact_type = armor_plate
					has_variable = valyrian_steel
				}
			}
			random_equipped_character_artifact = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						artifact_type = armor_plate
						has_variable = valyrian_steel
					}
					save_scope_as = valyrian_steel_armour
					scope:activity = {
						set_variable = {
							name = brought_vs_armour_from_home
							value = scope:valyrian_steel_armour
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.3304 = { # Failure
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.3304.t
	desc = agot_sailing.3304.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = anger
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:mutiny_leader
		animation = personality_callous
	}

	trigger = {
		has_available_guest = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		scope:activity = {
			random_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					is_alive = yes
					is_ai = yes
					has_relation_rival = root
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				alternative_limit = {
					is_alive = yes
					is_ai = yes
					opinion = {
						target = root
						value < -30
					}
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				alternative_limit = {
					is_alive = yes
					is_ai = yes
					opinion = {
						target = root
						value < 0
					}
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				alternative_limit = {
					is_alive = yes
					is_ai = yes
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				save_scope_as = mutiny_leader
			}
			every_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				trigger_event = agot_sailing.3929
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Failure - crew mutinies - walk the plank
		name = agot_sailing.3304.a
		death = { death_reason = death_drowned }
	}

agot_sailing.3306 = { # Success Cannibalism
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.3306.t
	desc = agot_sailing.3306.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = personality_callous
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:man_meat
		animation = poison
	}

	immediate = {
		scope:activity = {
			every_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						this = scope:host
						this = scope:man_meat
					}
				}
				trigger_event = agot_sailing.3307
			}
		}
		unknown_cannibal_murder_effect = {
			VICTIM = scope:man_meat
			MURDERER = scope:host
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_cannibal_trigger = no }
			add_secret = { type = secret_cannibal }
		}
	}

	option = { # Success
		name = agot_sailing.3306.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_secret = { type = secret_cannibal }
			}
			scope:activity = {
				every_attending_character = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { this = scope:host }
					}
					save_scope_as = guest
					scope:host = {
						every_secret = {
							type = secret_cannibal
							reveal_to = scope:guest
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:activity = {
			every_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				save_scope_as = guest
				scope:host = {
					every_secret = {
						limit = { secret_target = scope:man_meat }
						type = secret_murder
						reveal_to = scope:guest
					}
				}
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			compassionate = massive_stress_impact_gain
			cannibal = massive_stress_impact_loss
		}
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.3400
			days = 1
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.3307 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.3306.t
	desc = agot_sailing.3307.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = personality_callous
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:man_meat
		animation = poison
	}

	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.3307.a
		if = {
			limit = { is_cannibal_trigger = no }
			add_secret = { type = secret_cannibal }
		}
		scope:activity = {
			every_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { this = root }
				}
				save_scope_as = sailing_guest
				root = {
					every_secret = {
						type = secret_cannibal
						reveal_to = scope:sailing_guest
					}
				}
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			base = medium_stress_impact_gain
			cannibal = major_stress_impact_loss
			sadistic = major_stress_impact_loss
			callous = major_stress_impact_loss

			compassionate = massive_stress_impact_loss
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				has_trait = cannibal
				add = 100
			}
			ai_value_modifier = {
				ai_compassion = -0.5
				ai_honor = -0.5
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = compassionate
				add = -40
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.3307.b
		duel = {
			skill = stewardship
			value = valyria_skill_challenge
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					has_trait = temperate
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					has_trait = shrewd
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					has_trait = administrator
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					has_trait = inappetetic
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					has_trait = just
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
				}
				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.3307.b.success_tt
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 20
					has_trait = gluttonous
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					has_trait = greedy
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					has_trait = lifestyle_reveler
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					has_trait = comfort_eater
				}
				death = { death_reason = death_starved }
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			ai_value_modifier = {
				ai_compassion = 0.5
				ai_honor = 0.5
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = compassionate
				add = 40
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = cannibal
				factor = 0
			}
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.3501 = { # Success
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.3501.t
	desc = agot_sailing.3501.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = personality_content
	}

	immediate = {
		scope:activity = {
			every_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				trigger_event = agot_sailing.3932
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Success
		name = agot_sailing.3501.a
		# Mariner skill
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
				scope:activity = {
					add_activity_log_entry = {
						key = agot_sailing_completed_log
						tags = { good }
						# this line below adds the entry to the Effects section of the conclusion UI
						show_in_conclusion = yes
						character = root

						root = {
							if = {
								limit = {
									has_trait_xp = {
										trait = lifestyle_mariner
										value < 100
									}
								}
								xp_modifiers = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				scope:activity = {
					add_activity_log_entry = {
						key = agot_sailing_completed_log
						tags = { good }
						# this line below adds the entry to the Effects section of the conclusion UI
						show_in_conclusion = yes
						character = root

						root = { add_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.3900
			days = 1
		}
	}
}

				limit = {
					is_ai = no
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				trigger_event = agot_sailing.3933 # Kill event
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Failure
		name = agot_sailing.3502.a
		if = {
			limit = { has_access_to_a_ship = yes }
			remove_house_ship = yes
		}
		scope:activity = {
			every_attending_character = {
				limit = { is_ai = yes }
				death = { death_reason = death_drowned }
			}
			every_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				trigger_event = agot_sailing.3503
			}
			show_as_tooltip = {
				every_attending_character = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = no
						NOT = { this = scope:host }
					}
					death = { death_reason = death_drowned }
				}
			}
		}
		death = { death_reason = death_drowned }
	}
}

agot_sailing.3900 = { # Valyria Found
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.3900.t
	desc = agot_sailing.3900.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = fp1_beached_longships # Find something better maybe
	}
	override_effect_2d = {
		reference = legend_glow
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = happiness
	}

	immediate = {
		scope:activity = {
			every_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				trigger_event = agot_sailing.3934
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = reached_valyria }
			}
			add_character_modifier = reached_valyria
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_character_modifier = guest_reached_valyria }
					remove_character_modifier = guest_reached_valyria
				}
			}
			add_prestige = 1000
			if = {
				limit = { has_legitimacy = yes }
				add_legitimacy = medium_legitimacy_gain
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = guest_reached_valyria_tt
			scope:activity = {
				every_attending_character = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = guest_reached_valyria }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = reached_valyria }
						is_ai = yes
					}
					add_character_modifier = guest_reached_valyria
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Watch from a distance
		name = agot_sailing.3900.a
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.3900.a.tt
		trigger_event = agot_sailing.3901
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}

	option = { # Set foot on the ruins
		name = agot_sailing.3900.b

		random_list = {
			70 = {
				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.3900.b.success
				trigger_event = agot_sailing.3902
				hidden_effect = {
					random = {
						chance = 30
						scope:activity = {
							every_attending_character = {
								limit = {
									is_ai = no
									NOT = { this = scope:host }
								}
								trigger_event = agot_sailing.3905
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			30 = {
				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.3900.b.failure
				trigger_event = agot_sailing.3903
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
}

scripted_effect first_valyrian_artifact = {
	random_list = {
		# Candle, Horn, Mirror, Egg, Sword/Bow, Armor
		45 = { # 45%
			agot_create_random_glass_candle_effect = { OWNER = root }
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:activity = { root = scope:host }
				}
				scope:newly_created_artifact = { save_scope_as = candle_store }
			}
			else = {
				scope:newly_created_artifact = { save_scope_as = candle_store_stock }
			}
			add_character_flag = candle
		}
		10 = { # 10%
			if = {
				limit = { exists = global_var:binder_left_valyria }
				agot_create_artifact_dragon_horn_effect = { OWNER = root DRAGON = root }
			}
			else = {
				agot_create_artifact_dragonbinder_effect = { OWNER = root }
				set_global_variable = binder_left_valyria
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:activity = { root = scope:host }
				}
				scope:newly_created_artifact = { save_scope_as = candle_store }
			}
			else = {
				scope:newly_created_artifact = { save_scope_as = candle_store_stock }
			}
		}
		10 = { # 10%
			agot_create_artifact_generic_vs_mirror_effect = { OWNER = root }
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:activity = { root = scope:host }
				}
				scope:newly_created_artifact = { save_scope_as = candle_store }
			}
			else = {
				scope:newly_created_artifact = { save_scope_as = candle_store_stock }
			}
		}
		15 = { # 15%
			random_list = {
				1 = {
					character:dragon_ancestor_2 = { save_scope_as = dragon_parent }
				}
				1 = {
					character:dragon_ancestor_3 = { save_scope_as = dragon_parent }
				}
				1 = {
					character:dragon_ancestor_4 = { save_scope_as = dragon_parent }
				}
				1 = {
					character:dragon_pythax = { save_scope_as = dragon_parent }
				}
				0 = {
					modifier = {
						has_game_rule = agot_silly_mode
						add = 1
					}
					character:dragon_nagga = { save_scope_as = dragon_parent }
				}
			}
			agot_create_random_dragon_egg_artifact = { OWNER = root }
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:activity = { root = scope:host }
				}
				scope:newly_created_artifact = { save_scope_as = candle_store }
			}
			else = {
				scope:newly_created_artifact = { save_scope_as = candle_store_stock }
			}
		}
		15 = { # 15%
			valyria_weapon_found = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:activity = { root = scope:host }
				}
				scope:newly_created_artifact = { save_scope_as = candle_store }
			}
			else = {
				scope:newly_created_artifact = { save_scope_as = candle_store_stock }
			}
		}
		5 = { # 5%
			agot_create_artifact_valyrian_steel_armor_effect = { OWNER = root }
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:activity = { root = scope:host }
				}
				scope:newly_created_artifact = { save_scope_as = candle_store }
			}
			else = {
				scope:newly_created_artifact = { save_scope_as = candle_store_stock }
			}
		}
	}
}

scripted_effect second_valyrian_artifact = {
	scope:candle_store ?= { save_scope_as = backup }
	random_list = {
		# Candle, Horn, Mirror, Egg, Sword/Bow, Armor
		5 = { # 5%
			agot_create_random_glass_candle_effect = { OWNER = root }
			scope:newly_created_artifact = { save_scope_as = candle_store_2 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = candle }
				add_character_flag = candle_2
			}
			else = { add_character_flag = candle }
		}
		20 = { # 20%
			if = {
				limit = { exists = global_var:binder_left_valyria }
				agot_create_artifact_dragon_horn_effect = { OWNER = root DRAGON = root }
			}
			else = {
				agot_create_artifact_dragonbinder_effect = { OWNER = root }
				set_global_variable = binder_left_valyria
			}
			scope:newly_created_artifact = { save_scope_as = candle_store_2 }
		}
		5 = { # 5%
			agot_create_artifact_generic_vs_mirror_effect = { OWNER = root }
			scope:newly_created_artifact = { save_scope_as = candle_store_2 }
		}
		25 = { # 30%
			random_list = {
				1 = {
					character:dragon_ancestor_2 = { save_scope_as = dragon_parent }
				}
				1 = {
					character:dragon_ancestor_3 = { save_scope_as = dragon_parent }
				}
				1 = {
					character:dragon_ancestor_4 = { save_scope_as = dragon_parent }
				}
				1 = {
					character:dragon_pythax = { save_scope_as = dragon_parent }
				}
				0 = {
					modifier = {
						has_game_rule = agot_silly_mode
						add = 1
					}
					character:dragon_nagga = { save_scope_as = dragon_parent }
				}
			}
			agot_create_random_dragon_egg_artifact = { OWNER = root }
			scope:newly_created_artifact = { save_scope_as = candle_store_2 }
		}
		25 = { # 20%
			valyria_weapon_found = yes
			scope:newly_created_artifact = { save_scope_as = candle_store_2 }
		}
		10 = { # 10%
			agot_create_artifact_krakenhorn_generic_effect = { OWNER = root }
			scope:newly_created_artifact = { save_scope_as = candle_store_2 }
		}
		10 = { # 10%
			agot_create_artifact_valyrian_steel_armor_effect = { OWNER = root }
			scope:newly_created_artifact = { save_scope_as = candle_store_2 }
		}
	}
	scope:backup ?= { save_scope_as = candle_store }
}

agot_sailing.3902 = { # Setting Foot on Valyria - Success
	type = activity_event

		reference = fp1_beached_longships
	}

		reference = legend_glow
	}

		character = root
		animation = happiness
	}

	}

								id = agot_sailing.3904
								days = 1
							}
						}

								}
							}
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						has_character_flag = candle
						add = -25
					}
				}
				20 = {
					trigger_event = {
						id = agot_sailing.3907
						days = 1
					}
					scope:activity = {
						every_attending_character = {
							limit = {
								is_ai = no
								NOT = { this = scope:host }
							}
							trigger_event = agot_sailing.3936
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						has_character_flag = candle
						add = 25
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				has_trait = content
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = ambitious
				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = greedy
				add = -20
			}
		}
	}

	option = {

		trigger_event = agot_sailing.3906

scripted_effect destroy_crown = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_equipped_character_artifact = {
				AND = {
					artifact_type = helmet
					has_variable = historical_unique_artifact
				}
			}
		}
		random_equipped_character_artifact = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					artifact_type = helmet
					has_variable = historical_unique_artifact
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = lost_canon_crown
			valyria_recoverable_crown_lost = yes
			destroy_artifact = this
		}
	}
}

scripted_effect destroy_canon_armor = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_equipped_character_artifact = {
				AND = {
					artifact_type = armor_plate
					has_variable = historical_unique_artifact
				}
			}
		}
		random_equipped_character_artifact = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					artifact_type = armor_plate
					has_variable = historical_unique_artifact
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = lost_canon_armor
			valyria_recoverable_armor_lost = yes
			destroy_artifact = this
		}
	}
}

scripted_trigger not_candle_store_stock = {
	NOR = {
		this = scope:candle_store
		this = scope:candle_store_2
	}
}

scripted_effect death_of_crew = {
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:activity.var:brought_vs_armour_from_home }
		destroy_artifact = scope:activity.var:brought_vs_armour_from_home
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:activity.var:brought_vs_from_home
		}
		scope:activity.var:brought_vs_from_home ?= { save_scope_as = lost_valyrian_steel }
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:activity.var:brought_vs_from_home = { agot_is_recoverable_weapon = yes }
			}
			scope:activity.var:brought_vs_from_home = { save_scope_as = lost_valyrian_steel }
			valyria_recoverable_weapon_lost = yes
		}
		destroy_artifact = scope:lost_valyrian_steel
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_equipped_character_artifact = {
					has_variable = valyrian_steel
					OR = {
						artifact_type = sword
						artifact_type = axe
					}
					not_candle_store_stock = yes
				}
			}
			every_equipped_character_artifact = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = valyrian_steel
					OR = {
						artifact_type = sword
						artifact_type = axe
					}
					not_candle_store_stock = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = { agot_is_recoverable_weapon = yes }
					save_scope_as = lost_valyrian_steel
					valyria_recoverable_weapon_lost = yes
				}
				destroy_artifact = this
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_equipped_character_artifact = {
					has_variable = historical_unique_artifact
					not_candle_store_stock = yes
					OR = {
						artifact_type = hammer
						artifact_type = sword
						artifact_type = mace
						artifact_type = longbow
						artifact_type = axe
					}
				}
			}
			every_equipped_character_artifact = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = historical_unique_artifact
					not_candle_store_stock = yes
					OR = {
						artifact_type = hammer
						artifact_type = sword
						artifact_type = mace
						artifact_type = longbow
						artifact_type = axe
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { agot_is_recoverable_weapon = yes }
					save_scope_as = lost_valyrian_steel
					valyria_recoverable_weapon_lost = yes
				}
				destroy_artifact = this
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_equipped_character_artifact = {
					NOT = { has_variable = historical_unique_artifact }
					NOT = { has_variable = valyrian_steel }
					not_candle_store_stock = yes
					OR = {
						artifact_type = sword
						artifact_type = axe
						artifact_type = mace
						artifact_type = longbow
						artifact_type = hammer
						artifact_type = dagger
					}
				}
			}
			every_equipped_character_artifact = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_variable = historical_unique_artifact }
					NOT = { has_variable = valyrian_steel }
					not_candle_store_stock = yes
					OR = {
						artifact_type = sword
						artifact_type = axe
						artifact_type = mace
						artifact_type = longbow
						artifact_type = hammer
					}
				}
				destroy_artifact = this
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_equipped_character_artifact = {
					AND = {
						artifact_type = helmet
						has_variable = historical_unique_artifact
					}
				}
			}
			destroy_crown = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_equipped_character_artifact = {
					AND = {
						artifact_type = helmet
						has_variable = historical_unique_artifact
					}
				}
			}
			destroy_canon_armor = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_equipped_character_artifact = {
				exists = this
				NOR = {
					artifact_type = armor_plate
					artifact_type = sword
					artifact_type = axe
					artifact_type = mace
					artifact_type = longbow
					artifact_type = hammer
					artifact_type = dagger
					artifact_type = helmet
					ep1_artifact_is_court_artifact_trigger = yes
					artifact_type = throne_special
					artifact_type = dragon_skull
				}
				not_candle_store_stock = yes
			}
		}
		every_equipped_character_artifact = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					artifact_type = armor_plate
					artifact_type = sword
					artifact_type = axe
					artifact_type = mace
					artifact_type = longbow
					artifact_type = hammer
					artifact_type = helmet
					ep1_artifact_is_court_artifact_trigger = yes
					artifact_type = throne_special
					artifact_type = dragon_skull
				}
				not_candle_store_stock = yes
			}
			destroy_artifact = this
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { scope:lost_valyrian_steel ?= scope:candle_store }
		}
		destroy_artifact ?= scope:candle_store
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:candle_store_2 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { scope:lost_valyrian_steel ?= scope:candle_store_2 }
			}
			destroy_artifact ?= scope:candle_store_2
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:found_crown }
		scope:found_crown = {
			save_scope_as = lost_canon_crown
			valyria_recoverable_crown_lost = yes
			destroy_artifact = this
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:found_armour }
		scope:found_armour = {
			save_scope_as = lost_canon_armor
			valyria_recoverable_armor_lost = yes
			destroy_artifact = this
		}

		animation = happiness
	}
	artifact = {
		trigger = { exists = scope:candle_store_stock }
		target = scope:candle_store_stock
		position = lower_left_portrait
	}
	artifact = {
		trigger = { exists = scope:candle_store_2 }
		target = scope:candle_store_2
		position = lower_right_portrait
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = been_once }
			}
			first_valyrian_artifact = yes
			scope:candle_store_stock ?= { save_scope_as = newest_found_artifact }
		}
		else = {
			second_valyrian_artifact = yes
			scope:candle_store_2 ?= { save_scope_as = newest_found_artifact }
		}
		add_character_flag = been_once
	}

	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.3905.a
	}
}

agot_sailing.3907 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.3907.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_character_flag = writhing_wriggling }
				desc = agot_sailing.3907.desc_bad
			}
			desc = agot_sailing.3907.desc_good
		}
	}
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = fp1_beached_longships
	}
	override_effect_2d = {
		reference = legend_glow
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = root
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = {
				has_character_flag = writhing_wriggling
			}
			animation = poison
		}
		animation = happiness
	}
	artifact = {
		target = scope:candle_store
		position = lower_left_portrait
	}
	artifact = {
		trigger = { exists = scope:candle_store_2 }
		target = scope:candle_store_2
		position = lower_center_portrait
	}

	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = writhing_wriggling }
		}
		name = agot_sailing.3907.a_good
	}

	option = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = writhing_wriggling }
		name = agot_sailing.3907.a_bad
	}

	after = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = writhing_wriggling }
			if = {
				limit = { has_access_to_a_ship = yes }
				remove_house_ship = yes
			}
			death_of_crew = yes
			hidden_effect = {
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = { is_ai = yes }
						death = { death_reason = death_fire_worms }
					}
				}
			}
			show_as_tooltip = {
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						death = { death_reason = death_fire_worms }
					}
				}
			}
			death = { death_reason = death_fire_worms }
		}
		else = {
			trigger_event = {
				id = agot_sailing.9001
				days = 15
			}
		}
	}
}

			lazy = medium_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.5500 = { # Setting out for the hunt
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.5500.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					has_character_flag = sailing_looking_new_prey
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.5500.desc.a
			}
			desc = agot_sailing.5500.desc
		}
	}
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = thinking
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:first_mate
		animation = throne_room_two_handed_passive_1
	}
	trigger = {
		exists = scope:activity
	}
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:first_mate = { is_alive = no }
			}
			set_first_mate = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_turtle_sighted }
			remove_character_flag = sailing_turtle_sighted
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_seal_sighted }
			remove_character_flag = sailing_seal_sighted
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_dolphin_sighted }
			remove_character_flag = sailing_dolphin_sighted
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_walrus_sighted }
			remove_character_flag = sailing_walrus_sighted
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_orca_sighted }
			remove_character_flag = sailing_orca_sighted
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_humpback_sighted }
			remove_character_flag = sailing_humpback_sighted
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_spermwhale_sighted }
			remove_character_flag = sailing_spermwhale_sighted
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_bluewhale_sighted }
			remove_character_flag = sailing_bluewhale_sighted
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_kraken_sighted }
			remove_character_flag = sailing_kraken_sighted
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_whitedread_sighted }
			remove_character_flag = sailing_whitedread_sighted
		}
	}
	option = { # All right, let's keep our eyes open!
		name = agot_sailing.5500.a
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.5500.a.tt
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				15 = { # A turtle
					add_character_flag = sailing_turtle_sighted
				}
				10 = { # A seal
					add_character_flag = sailing_seal_sighted
				}
				10 = { # A dolphin
					add_character_flag = sailing_dolphin_sighted
				}
				10 = { # A walrus
					add_character_flag = sailing_walrus_sighted
				}
				10 = { # An orca
					add_character_flag = sailing_orca_sighted
				}
				10 = { # A humpback whale
					add_character_flag = sailing_humpback_sighted
				}
				10 = { # A sperm whale
					add_character_flag = sailing_spermwhale_sighted
				}
				10 = { # A blue whale
					add_character_flag = sailing_bluewhale_sighted
				}
				10 = { # A sea drake
					add_character_flag = sailing_kraken_sighted
				}
				5 = { # The legendary White Dread!
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = white_dread_slain }
						add_character_flag = sailing_spermwhale_sighted
					}
					else = {
						add_character_flag = sailing_whitedread_sighted
					}
					modifier = {
						add = 10
						has_character_modifier = the_white_beast_modifier
					}
				}
			}
			trigger_event = {
				id = agot_sailing.5501
				days = 5
			}
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.5501 = { # Look for signs of a creature!
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.5501.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_character_flag = sailing_turtle_sighted }
				desc = agot_sailing.5501.desc.a
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_character_flag = sailing_seal_sighted }
				desc = agot_sailing.5501.desc.b
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_character_flag = sailing_dolphin_sighted }
				desc = agot_sailing.5501.desc.c
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_character_flag = sailing_walrus_sighted }
				desc = agot_sailing.5501.desc.d
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_character_flag = sailing_orca_sighted }
				desc = agot_sailing.5501.desc.e
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_character_flag = sailing_humpback_sighted }
				desc = agot_sailing.5501.desc.f
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_character_flag = sailing_spermwhale_sighted }
				desc = agot_sailing.5501.desc.g
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_character_flag = sailing_bluewhale_sighted }
				desc = agot_sailing.5501.desc.h
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_character_flag = sailing_kraken_sighted }
				desc = agot_sailing.5501.desc.i
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					has_character_flag = sailing_whitedread_sighted
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = the_white_beast_modifier }
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.5501.desc.j
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = sailing_whitedread_sighted
						has_character_modifier = the_white_beast_modifier
					}
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.5501.desc.k
			}
		}
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = { var:reroll >= 3 }
			desc = agot_sailing.5501.reroll
		}
	}
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = throne_room_two_handed_passive_1
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:first_mate
		animation = throne_room_one_handed_passive_1
	}
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:first_mate = { is_alive = no }
			}
			set_first_mate = yes
		}
		scope:activity = {
			every_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				trigger_event = agot_sailing.5600
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = var:reroll }
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = reroll
				value = 0
			}
		}
		else = {
			increase_variable = {
				NAME = reroll
				AMOUNT = 1
			}
		}
		scope:activity = {
			random_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				save_scope_as = rando
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # After it! Harpoons at the ready!
		name = agot_sailing.5501.a
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.5501.a.tt
		trigger = { has_character_flag = sailing_turtle_sighted }
		trigger_event = agot_sailing.5502
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = { # After it! Harpoons at the ready!
		name = agot_sailing.5501.b
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.5501.b.tt
		trigger = { has_character_flag = sailing_seal_sighted }
		trigger_event = agot_sailing.5503
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = { # After it! Harpoons at the ready!
		name = agot_sailing.5501.c
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.5501.c.tt
		trigger = { has_character_flag = sailing_dolphin_sighted }
		trigger_event = agot_sailing.5504
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = { # After it! Harpoons at the ready!
		name = agot_sailing.5501.d
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.5501.d.tt
		trigger = { has_character_flag = sailing_walrus_sighted }
		trigger_event = agot_sailing.5505
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = { # After it! Harpoons at the ready!
		name = agot_sailing.5501.e
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.5501.e.tt
		trigger = { has_character_flag = sailing_orca_sighted }
		trigger_event = agot_sailing.5506
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = { # After it! Harpoons at the ready!
		name = agot_sailing.5501.f
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.5501.f.tt
		trigger = { has_character_flag = sailing_humpback_sighted }
		trigger_event = agot_sailing.5507
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = { # After it! Harpoons at the ready!
		name = agot_sailing.5501.g
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.5501.g.tt
		trigger = { has_character_flag = sailing_spermwhale_sighted }
		trigger_event = agot_sailing.5508
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = { # After it! Harpoons at the ready!
		name = agot_sailing.5501.h
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.5501.h.tt
		trigger = { has_character_flag = sailing_bluewhale_sighted }
		trigger_event = agot_sailing.5509
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = { # After it! Harpoons at the ready!

		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.5501.i.tt
		trigger = { has_character_flag = sailing_kraken_sighted }

		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.5501.j.tt
		trigger = { has_character_flag = sailing_whitedread_sighted }

		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = { # We'll look for better prey.

		}

					has_character_flag = sailing_whitedread_sighted
				}
				has_trait = craven
			}
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.5502 = { # Try to catch the turtle
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.5502.t
	desc = agot_sailing.5502.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = aggressive_spear
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:first_mate
		animation = throne_room_cheer_1
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:first_mate = { is_alive = no }
			}
			set_first_mate = yes
		}
	}
	option = { # Ready, aim, and...
		name = agot_sailing.5502.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent }
		}
		random_list = {
			75 = {
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 20
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 25
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
				}
				stress_impact = {
					brave = minor_stress_impact_loss
					wrathful = minor_stress_impact_loss
					lifestyle_mariner = major_stress_impact_loss
					sadistic = minor_stress_impact_loss

					shy = medium_stress_impact_gain
					lazy = medium_stress_impact_gain
					compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
				}
				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.5502.a.success
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_sailing.9001
					days = 5
				}
				send_interface_toast = {
					left_icon = root
					title = agot_sailing.5502.a.success
					custom_tooltip = sailing.hunt.success
					add_gold = minor_gold_value
					add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:host = { exists = domicile }
					}
					scope:host = {
						domicile = { change_provisions = miniscule_provisions_gain }
					}
				}
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.5601
					}
				}
			}
			25 = {
				modifier = {
					add = -10
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -20
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -25
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
				}
				stress_impact = {
					brave = minor_stress_impact_gain
					wrathful = minor_stress_impact_gain
					lifestyle_mariner = major_stress_impact_gain
					sadistic = minor_stress_impact_gain

					shy = medium_stress_impact_loss
					lazy = medium_stress_impact_loss
					compassionate = minor_stress_impact_loss
				}
				custom_tooltip = sailing.hunt.failure.tt
				send_interface_toast = {
					left_icon = root
					title = sailing.hunt.failure.tt
					custom_tooltip = sailing.hunt.failure
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_sailing.9002
					days = 5
				}
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.5602
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Ready, aim, and...
		trigger = { has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent }
		name = agot_sailing.5502.a
		random_list = {
			75 = {
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 20
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 25
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.5502.a.success
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_sailing.9001
					days = 5
				}
				send_interface_toast = {
					left_icon = root
					title = agot_sailing.5502.a.success
					custom_tooltip = sailing.hunt.success
					add_gold = minor_gold_value
					add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = lifestyle_mariner
								value < 100
							}
						}
						add_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_mariner
							value = 5
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					random = {
						chance = 75
						add_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:host = { exists = domicile }
					}
					scope:host = {
						domicile = { change_provisions = miniscule_provisions_gain }
					}
				}
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.5601
					}
				}
			}
			25 = {
				modifier = {
					add = -10
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -20
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -25
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = sailing.hunt.failure.tt
				send_interface_toast = {
					left_icon = root
					title = sailing.hunt.failure.tt
					custom_tooltip = sailing.hunt.failure
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = lifestyle_mariner
								value < 100
							}
						}
						add_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_mariner
							value = 1
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_sailing.9002
					days = 5
				}
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.5602
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

agot_sailing.5503 = { # Try to catch the seal
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.5503.t
	desc = agot_sailing.5503.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = hunting_longbow_aim_arrow_default
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:first_mate
		animation = throne_room_cheer_1
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:first_mate = { is_alive = no }
			}
			set_first_mate = yes
		}
	}
	option = { # Ready, aim, and...
		name = agot_sailing.5503.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent }
		}
		random_list = {
			70 = {
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 20
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 30
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
				}
				stress_impact = {
					brave = minor_stress_impact_loss
					wrathful = minor_stress_impact_loss
					lifestyle_mariner = major_stress_impact_loss
					sadistic = minor_stress_impact_loss

					shy = medium_stress_impact_gain
					lazy = medium_stress_impact_gain
					compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
				}
				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.5503.a.success
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_sailing.9001
					days = 5
				}
				send_interface_toast = {
					left_icon = root
					title = agot_sailing.5503.a.success
					custom_tooltip = sailing.hunt.success
					add_gold = minor_gold_value
					add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:host = { exists = domicile }
					}
					scope:host = {
						domicile = { change_provisions = miniscule_provisions_gain }
					}
				}
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.5601
					}
				}
			}
			30 = {
				modifier = {
					add = -10
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -20
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -30
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
				}
				stress_impact = {
					brave = minor_stress_impact_gain
					wrathful = minor_stress_impact_gain
					lifestyle_mariner = major_stress_impact_gain
					sadistic = minor_stress_impact_gain

					shy = medium_stress_impact_loss
					lazy = medium_stress_impact_loss
					compassionate = minor_stress_impact_loss
				}
				custom_tooltip = sailing.hunt.failure.tt
				send_interface_toast = {
					left_icon = root
					title = sailing.hunt.failure.tt
					custom_tooltip = sailing.hunt.failure
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_sailing.9002
					days = 5
				}
				scope:activity = {

						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.5602
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Ready, aim, and...
		trigger = { has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent }
		name = agot_sailing.5503.a
		random_list = {
			70 = {

					add = 10
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 20

						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}

					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}

				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.5503.a.success

					add_gold = minor_gold_value
					add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = lifestyle_mariner
								value < 100
							}

						add_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_mariner
							value = 5
						}
					}
				}
				else = {

					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {

						domicile = { change_provisions = miniscule_provisions_gain }
					}
				}
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.5601
					}
				}

			30 = {
				modifier = {
					add = -10
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -20
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -30
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = sailing.hunt.failure.tt

					if = {

								value < 100
							}
						}

						}
					}
				}

					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -30
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = sailing.hunt.failure.tt
				send_interface_toast = {
					left_icon = root
					title = sailing.hunt.failure.tt
					custom_tooltip = sailing.hunt.failure
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = lifestyle_mariner
								value < 100
							}
						}
						add_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_mariner
							value = 5
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					random = {
						chance = 75
						add_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					}
				}
				random = {
					chance = 50
					increase_wounds_no_death_effect = { REASON = fight}
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_sailing.9002
					days = 5
				}
				scope:activity = {

						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.5602

agot_sailing.5506 = { # Try to catch the orca
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.5506.t
	desc = agot_sailing.5506.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = aggressive_spear
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:first_mate
		animation = personality_compassionate
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:first_mate = { is_alive = no }
			}
			set_first_mate = yes
		}
	}
	option = { # Ready, aim, and...
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent }
		}
		name = agot_sailing.5506.a
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 20
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 30
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.5506.a.success
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_sailing.9001
					days = 5
				}
				stress_impact = {
					brave = minor_stress_impact_loss
					wrathful = minor_stress_impact_loss
					lifestyle_mariner = major_stress_impact_loss
					sadistic = minor_stress_impact_loss

					shy = medium_stress_impact_gain
					lazy = medium_stress_impact_gain
					compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
				}
				send_interface_toast = {
					left_icon = root
					title = agot_sailing.5506.a.success
					custom_tooltip = sailing.hunt.success
					add_gold = major_gold_value
					add_prestige = major_prestige_gain
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:host = { exists = domicile }
					}
					scope:host = {
						domicile = { change_provisions = medium_provisions_gain }
					}
				}
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.5601
					}
				}
			}
			50 = {
				modifier = {
					add = -10
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -20
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -30
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = sailing.hunt.failure.tt
				stress_impact = {
					brave = minor_stress_impact_gain
					wrathful = minor_stress_impact_gain
					lifestyle_mariner = major_stress_impact_gain
					sadistic = minor_stress_impact_gain

					shy = medium_stress_impact_loss
					lazy = medium_stress_impact_loss
					compassionate = minor_stress_impact_loss
				}
				send_interface_toast = {
					left_icon = root
					title = sailing.hunt.failure.tt
					custom_tooltip = sailing.hunt.failure
				}
				random = {
					chance = 50
					increase_wounds_no_death_effect = { REASON = fight}
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_sailing.9002
					days = 5
				}
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.5602
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

								trait = lifestyle_mariner
								value < 100
							}
						}
						add_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_mariner
							value = 5
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					random = {
						chance = 75
						add_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:host = { exists = domicile }
					}
					scope:host = {
						domicile = { change_provisions = massive_provisions_gain }
					}
				}
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.5601
					}
				}
			}
			60 = {
				modifier = {
					add = -10
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -20
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -30
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = sailing.hunt.failure.tt
				send_interface_toast = {
					left_icon = root
					title = sailing.hunt.failure.tt
					custom_tooltip = sailing.hunt.failure
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = lifestyle_mariner
								value < 100
							}
						}
						add_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_mariner
							value = 5
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					random = {
						chance = 75
						add_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					}
				}
				random = {
					chance = 50
					increase_wounds_effect = { REASON = fight }
				}

				modifier = {
					add = -20
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -35
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -5
					has_character_modifier = the_white_beast_modifier
				}
				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.5511.a.failure
				send_interface_toast = {
					left_icon = root
					right_icon = scope:rando
					title = agot_sailing.5511.a.failure
					custom_tooltip = sailing.hunt.failure
					random = {
						chance = 50
						scope:rando = {
							death = { death_reason = death_drowned }
						}
					}
				}
				random = {
					chance = 95
					increase_wounds_no_death_effect = { REASON = fight }
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_sailing.5512
					days = 5
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Ready, aim, and...
		name = agot_sailing.5511.a
		trigger = { has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent }
		random_list = {
			5 = {
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 20
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 35
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 5
					has_character_modifier = the_white_beast_modifier
				}
				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.5511.a.success
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_sailing.9001
					days = 5
				}
				send_interface_toast = {
					left_icon = root
					title = agot_sailing.5511.a.success
					custom_tooltip = sailing.hunt.success
					add_gold = monumental_gold_value
					add_prestige = massive_prestige_gain
					if = {
						limit = { has_legitimacy = yes }
						add_legitimacy = major_legitimacy_gain
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = lifestyle_mariner
								value < 100
							}
						}
						add_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_mariner
							value = 20
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					random = {
						chance = 75
						add_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:host = { exists = domicile }
					}
					scope:host = {
						domicile = { change_provisions = massive_provisions_gain }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_character_modifier = the_white_beast_modifier }
					remove_character_modifier = the_white_beast_modifier
				}
				add_character_flag = white_dread_slain
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.5601
					}
				}
			}
			95 = {
				modifier = {
					add = -10
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -20
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -35
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -5
					has_character_modifier = the_white_beast_modifier
				}
				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.5511.a.failure
				send_interface_toast = {

					custom_tooltip = sailing.hunt.failure
					random = {

							death = { death_reason = death_drowned }
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
