agot_sailing.5512 = { # the White Dread escapes you
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.5512.t
	desc = agot_sailing.5512.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = rage
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:first_mate
		animation = fear
	}

	override_effect_2d = {
		reference = fog
	}
	immediate = {
		scope:activity = {
			every_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				trigger_event = agot_sailing.5603
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:first_mate = { is_alive = no }
			}
			set_first_mate = yes
		}
	}
	option = { # I MUST SLAY IT!
		name = agot_sailing.5512.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = the_white_beast_modifier }
			}
			add_character_modifier = the_white_beast_modifier
		}
		stress_impact = {
			ambitious = minor_stress_impact_loss
			brave = minor_stress_impact_loss
			wrathful = minor_stress_impact_loss
			shy = medium_stress_impact_gain
			craven = major_stress_impact_gain
			lazy = medium_stress_impact_gain
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				has_trait = ambitious
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = wrathful
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = lazy
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = compassionate
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = craven
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = brave
				add = 10
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # No... I have to let go!
		name = agot_sailing.5512.b
		stress_impact = {
			base = minor_stress_impact_gain
			ambitious = major_stress_impact_gain
			brave = medium_stress_impact_gain
			wrathful = medium_stress_impact_gain
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_loss
			craven = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				has_trait = ambitious
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = wrathful
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = lazy
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = compassionate
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = craven
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = brave
				add = -10
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Failure is a great teacher!
		trigger = { has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent }
		name = agot_sailing.5512.c
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
				}
				add_trait_xp = {
					trait = lifestyle_mariner
					value = 5
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			random = {
				chance = 75
				add_trait = lifestyle_mariner
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
		}
	}
	after = {
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.9002
			days = 5
		}
	}
}

				}
				desc = agot_sailing.5601.desc.i
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					scope:host = { has_character_flag = sailing_kraken_sighted }
					scope:host = { has_character_flag = used_horn_hunting }
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.5601.desc.i_two
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					scope:host = { has_character_flag = sailing_whitedread_sighted }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = the_white_beast_modifier }
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.5601.desc.j
			}
			triggered_desc = {

					scope:host = { has_character_flag = sailing_whitedread_sighted }
					root = { has_character_modifier = the_white_beast_modifier }
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.5601.desc.k
			}
		}
	}
	theme = sailing_activity

		reference = ocean
	}

		animation = happiness
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = happiness
	}
	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.5601.a

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:host = { has_character_flag = sailing_whitedread_sighted }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						has_trait = compassionate
						has_trait = craven
					}
				}
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = impressed_opinion
					target = scope:host
					opinion = 40
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						has_trait = compassionate
						has_trait = craven
					}
				}
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = impressed_opinion
					target = scope:host
					opinion = 80
				}
			}
		}
	}

agot_sailing.6000 = {
	hidden = yes
	trigger = {
		exists = scope:previous_controller.capital_county
		scope:barony.title_province = scope:previous_controller.capital_county.title_province
		scope:previous_controller = {
			any_character_artifact = {
				has_variable = sunken_ship
				NOT = { has_variable = used_map_here }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		scope:previous_controller = {
			random_character_artifact = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sunken_ship
					NOT = { has_variable = used_map_here }
				}
				save_scope_as = map
				set_owner = root
			}
		}
		scope:map.var:sunken_ship = { save_scope_as = sunken_ship_province }
		send_interface_toast = {
			left_icon = root
			title = agot_sailing.6000.toast
			custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.6000.toast_desc
		}
	}
}

### One event to search the area from above, one event to dive in, one of three events to approach the wreckage (Stealth, learning and marital, or fight, prowess, through Sharks. Easy way there and they spot a massive White Whale go by. Swimming through a hard to maneuver area with limited breath to open it up to get the loot out )

agot_sailing.6001 = { # Searching for signs of the wreck
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.6001.t
	desc = agot_sailing.6001.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = survey
	}

	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:first_mate
		animation = steward
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:first_mate = { is_alive = no }
			}
			set_first_mate = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.6001.a
		random_list = {
			75 = { # Success
				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.6001.a.success.tt
				trigger_event = agot_sailing.6002
				random_character_artifact = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = sunken_ship
						var:sunken_ship = scope:activity.activity_location
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = character_used_map_here
						value = root
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent
						OR = {
							NOT = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
							AND = {
								has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
								has_trait_xp = {
									trait = lifestyle_mariner
									value < 100
								}
							}
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
							add_trait_xp = {
								trait = lifestyle_mariner
								value = 5
							}
					}
					else = { add_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
				}
				modifier = {
					has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
					add = 5
				}
				modifier = {
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
					add = 10
				}
				modifier = {
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
					add = 15
				}
				modifier = {
					any_character_artifact = {
						has_variable = sunken_ship
						var:sunken_ship = scope:activity.activity_location
						has_variable = character_used_map_here
						var:character_used_map_here = root
					}
					add = 10
				}
			}
			25 = { # Failure
				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.6001.a.failure.tt
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_sailing.9002
					days = 5
				}
				modifier = {
					has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
					add = -5
				}
				modifier = {
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 50
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 100
					}
					add = -10
				}
				modifier = {
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
					add = -15
				}
				modifier = {
					any_character_artifact = {
						has_variable = sunken_ship
						var:sunken_ship = scope:activity.activity_location
						has_variable = character_used_map_here
						var:character_used_map_here = root
					}
					add = -10
				}
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }
						}
						trigger_event = agot_sailing.6007
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.6002 = { # Diving down into the depth
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.6002.t
	desc = agot_sailing.6002.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}

		character = root
		outfit_tags = { nightgown }
	}

		character = scope:first_mate
		animation = steward
	}

	immediate = {
		add_character_flag = sailing_single_combat_stripped_to_waist
		scope:activity = {
			every_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				trigger_event = agot_sailing.6013
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:first_mate = { is_alive = no }
			}
			set_first_mate = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.6002.a
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.6003
			days = 2
		}
		stress_impact = {
			shy = minor_stress_impact_gain
			craven = minor_stress_impact_gain
			greedy = medium_stress_impact_loss
			brave = medium_stress_impact_loss
			eccentric = medium_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.6002.b
		custom_tooltip = agot_sailing_end

			days = 5

agot_sailing.6010 = { # Guest ending
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.6010.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {

			}
			triggered_desc = {

				desc = agot_sailing.6010.desc_normal
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_character_flag = small_stash }
				desc = agot_sailing.6010.desc_small
			}
			triggered_desc = {

	}
	theme = sailing_activity

		}

			add_gold = 125
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = small_stash }
			add_gold = 50
		}
	}

		name = agot_sailing.6010.a
		if = {
			limit = { has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent }
			if = {
				limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_mariner
							value < 100
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value = 5
					}
				}
			}
			else = { add_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
		}
	}

	after = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = big_stash }
			remove_character_flag = big_stash
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = normal_stash }
			remove_character_flag = normal_stash
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = small_stash }
			remove_character_flag = small_stash
		}
		else = { remove_character_flag = no_stash }
	}
}

agot_sailing.6011 = {
	hidden = yes

	immediate = {
		character:dragon_vermax = { save_scope_as = vermax }
		scope:activity.activity_location = { save_scope_as = treasure_location }
		treasure_trove_artifacts = { LOCATION = scope:treasure_location }
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:first_mate = { is_alive = no }
			}
			set_first_mate = yes
		}
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.6014
			days = 1
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.6012 = { # Player failure ending
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.6012.t
	desc = agot_sailing.6012.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = root
		outfit_tags = { nightgown }
		animation = sadness
	}

	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:first_mate
		animation = anger
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:first_mate = { is_alive = no }
			}
			set_first_mate = yes
		}
		scope:activity = {
			every_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { this = scope:host }
				}
				add_character_flag = no_stash
				trigger_event = agot_sailing.6010
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:activity.activity_location ?= {
					NOT = { has_province_modifier = sunken_ship_sighted }
				}
			}
			random_character_artifact = {
				limit = { has_variable = used_map_here }
				remove_variable = used_map_here
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.6012.a
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.9002
			days = 1
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.6014 = { # Player successful ending
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.6014.t
	desc = {
		desc = agot_sailing.6014.desc
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = { exists = scope:vermax_skull }
			desc = agot_sailing.6014.vermax_skull
		}
	}
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = root
		outfit_tags = { nightgown }
		animation = happiness
	}

	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:first_mate
		animation = happiness
	}

	artifact = {
		target = scope:vermax_skull
		position = lower_left_portrait
		trigger = { exists = scope:vermax_skull }
	}

	artifact = {
		target = scope:generic_artifact
		position = lower_right_portrait
		trigger = { exists = scope:generic_artifact }
	}

	artifact = {
		target = scope:valyrian_steel_sword
		position = lower_right_portrait
		trigger = { exists = scope:valyrian_steel_sword }
	}

	immediate = {
		treasure_trove = { LOCATION = scope:treasure_location }
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:generic_artifact }
			custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.6014.a_generic_artifact
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:valyrian_steel_sword }
			custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.6014.a_valyrian_steel_sword
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:spare }
			custom_tooltip = agot_sailing.6014.a_spare
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:vermax_skull }
			show_as_tooltip = {
				agot_create_artifact_dragon_skull_effect = {
					OWNER = this
					DRAGON = character:dragon_vermax
					KILLER = character:dragon_vermax
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = found_horn }
			show_as_tooltip = {
				agot_create_artifact_krakenhorn_generic_effect = { OWNER = root }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:treasure_location = {
					NOT = { has_province_modifier = sunken_ship_sighted }
				}
			}
			random_character_artifact = {
				limit = { has_variable = used_map_here }
				destroy_artifact = this
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.6014.a
		if = {
			limit = { has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent }
			if = {
				limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_mariner
							value < 100
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value = 5
					}
				}
			}
			else = { add_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
		}
		trigger_event = {
			id = agot_sailing.9001
			days = 1
		}
	}
}

############################
## Wrap-up events
## 9000-9999
############################

#9001: successful expedition
#9002: unsuccessful expedition
#9003: Management event (hidden)

agot_sailing.9001 = { # Successful mission
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.9001.t
	desc = agot_sailing.9001.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = docks
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = happiness
	}

	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:first_mate
		animation = happiness
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:first_mate = { is_alive = no }
			}
			set_first_mate = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_martial_lifestyle_xp = minor_lifestyle_xp
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_mariner
							value >= 100
						}
					}
				}

				# Mariner skill
				if = {
					limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
					scope:activity = {
						add_activity_log_entry = {
							key = gained_sailor_xp_log
							tags = { good }
							# this line below adds the entry to the Effects section of the conclusion UI
							show_in_conclusion = yes
							character = root

							root = {
								if = {
									limit = {
										has_trait_xp = {
											trait = lifestyle_mariner
											value < 100
										}
									}
									xp_modifiers = yes
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					scope:activity = {
						add_activity_log_entry = {
							key = gained_sailor_trait_log
							tags = { good }
							# this line below adds the entry to the Effects section of the conclusion UI
							show_in_conclusion = yes
							character = root

							root = { add_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = survived_valyria }
				root.dynasty ?= {
					add_dynasty_prestige = 1000
					add_dynasty_prestige_level = 1
				}
				remove_character_flag = survived_valyria
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # I'm back!
		name = agot_sailing.9001.a
		if = { # Gain extra rewards through adventurer dynasty legacy
			limit = {
				dynasty ?= { has_dynasty_perk = fp1_adventure_legacy_1 }
			}
			add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
			if = {
				limit = { has_legitimacy = yes }
				add_legitimacy = minor_legitimacy_gain
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		if = { # Gain extra rewards through adventurer dynasty legacy
			limit = {
				dynasty ?= { has_dynasty_perk = fp1_adventure_legacy_1 }
			}

			root = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_mariner
							value < 100
						}
					}
					xp_modifiers = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent
				has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
				has_trait_xp = {
					trait = lifestyle_mariner
					value >= root.var:sailing_xp_required
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = intent_completer
			send_interface_toast = {
				title = activity_intent_complete_toast
				left_icon = root
				complete_activity_intent = yes
			}
		}
		#End the activity
		trigger_event = agot_sailing.9003
	}
}

agot_sailing.9002 = { # UNSUCCESSFUL EXPEDITION / FALLBACK
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.9002.t
	desc = agot_sailing.9002.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = docks
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = sadness
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:first_mate
		animation = sadness
	}
	trigger = {
		exists = scope:activity
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:first_mate = { is_alive = no }
			}
			set_first_mate = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			# Mariner skill
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_mariner
							value >= 100
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
					scope:activity = {
						add_activity_log_entry = {
							key = agot_sailing_completed_log
							tags = { good }
							# this line below adds the entry to the Effects section of the conclusion UI
							show_in_conclusion = yes
							character = root

							root = {
								if = {
									limit = {
										has_trait_xp = {
											trait = lifestyle_mariner
											value < 100
										}
									}
									xp_modifiers = yes
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					scope:activity = {
						add_activity_log_entry = {
							key = agot_sailing_completed_log
							tags = { good }
							# this line below adds the entry to the Effects section of the conclusion UI
							show_in_conclusion = yes
							character = root

							root = { add_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:activity = {
					has_activity_option = { category = special_type option = agot_sailing_type_valyria }
				}
			}
			add_character_flag = failed_valyrian_mission
		}
	}

	option = { # I'm back!
		name = agot_sailing.9002.a
		if = { # Gain extra rewards through adventurer dynasty legacy
			limit = {
				dynasty ?= { has_dynasty_perk = fp1_adventure_legacy_1 }
			}
			add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
			if = {
				limit = { has_legitimacy = yes }
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:activity = {
							NOT = {
								has_activity_option = { category = special_type option = agot_sailing_type_valyria }
							}
						}
					}
					add_legitimacy = miniscule_legitimacy_gain
				}
				else = { add_legitimacy = medium_legitimacy_loss }
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		if = { # Gain extra rewards through adventurer dynasty legacy
			limit = {
				dynasty ?= { has_dynasty_perk = fp1_adventure_legacy_1 }
			}

			root = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_mariner
							value < 100
						}
					}
					xp_modifiers = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent
				has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_mariner
							value < 100
						}
					}
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= root.var:sailing_xp_required
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = intent_completer
			send_interface_toast = {
				title = activity_intent_complete_toast
				left_icon = root
				complete_activity_intent = yes
			}
		}
		#End the activity
		trigger_event = agot_sailing.9003
	}
}

agot_sailing.9003 = { # WRAP-UP EVENT
	hidden = yes
	immediate = {
		scope:activity = {
			every_attending_character = {
				# Mariner skill
				if = {
					limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = lifestyle_mariner
								value < 100
							}
						}
						xp_modifiers = yes
					}
				}
				else = {
					add_trait = lifestyle_mariner
				}
			}
		}

		agot_sailing_completed_log_entry_effect = yes
		scope:host = {
			#Boats
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = small_boat_modifier }
				remove_character_modifier = small_boat_modifier
				remove_character_flag = hired_a_boat
			}
			#Mission type
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_exploration_mission }
				remove_character_flag = sailing_exploration_mission
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_piracy_mission }
				remove_character_flag = sailing_piracy_mission
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_hunt_a_creature_misson }
				remove_character_flag = sailing_hunt_a_creature_misson
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_treasure_mission }
				remove_character_flag = sailing_treasure_mission
			}
			#Hunting
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_looking_new_prey }
				remove_character_flag = sailing_looking_new_prey
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_turtle_sighted }
				remove_character_flag = sailing_turtle_sighted
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_seal_sighted }
				remove_character_flag = sailing_seal_sighted
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_dolphin_sighted }
				remove_character_flag = sailing_dolphin_sighted
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_walrus_sighted }
				remove_character_flag = sailing_walrus_sighted
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_orca_sighted }
				remove_character_flag = sailing_orca_sighted
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_humpback_sighted }
				remove_character_flag = sailing_humpback_sighted
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_spermwhale_sighted }
				remove_character_flag = sailing_spermwhale_sighted
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_bluewhale_sighted }
				remove_character_flag = sailing_bluewhale_sighted
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_kraken_sighted }
				remove_character_flag = sailing_kraken_sighted
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_whitedread_sighted }
				remove_character_flag = sailing_whitedread_sighted
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = used_horn_hunting }
				remove_character_flag = used_horn_hunting
			}
			if = {
				limit = { exists = var:reroll }
				remove_variable = reroll
			}
			#Treasure Hunting
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_single_combat_stripped_to_waist }
				remove_character_flag = sailing_single_combat_stripped_to_waist
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_white_whale_blessing }
				remove_character_flag = sailing_white_whale_blessing
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sailing_white_whale_combat_blessing }
				remove_character_flag = sailing_white_whale_combat_blessing
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = treasure_hunt }
				remove_character_flag = treasure_hunt
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = found_horn }
				remove_character_flag = found_horn
			}
			#Valyria
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = used_horn }
				remove_character_flag = used_horn
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = candle }
				remove_character_flag = candle
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = candle_2 }
				remove_character_flag = candle_2
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = traveling_to_valyria }
				remove_character_flag = traveling_to_valyria
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = failed_valyrian_mission }
				remove_character_flag = failed_valyrian_mission
			}
			#XP Intent
			remove_variable = sailing_xp_required
		}
		scope:activity = {
			hidden_effect = { skip_activity_phase = yes }
		}
	}
}

### 9114: Arrives at the coast

### 9120: Merchant ship encounter

### 9130: Another pirate ship encounter
### 9131: Duel won to the pirate leader

###########################
agot_sailing.9100 = { # Select a target
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.9100.t
	desc = agot_sailing.9100.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = happiness
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:first_mate
		animation = personality_greedy
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:first_mate = { is_alive = no }
			}
			set_first_mate = yes

		name = agot_sailing.9100.a

		}

agot_sailing.9110 = { # Coastal Provinces Raid
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.9110.t
	desc = agot_sailing.9110.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = fp1_beached_longships
	}

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = rage
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:first_mate
		animation = marshal
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:first_mate = { is_alive = no }
			}
			set_first_mate = yes
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				is_coastal = yes
				county.holder = { is_human = yes }
				NOR = {
					culture = root.location.culture
					culture = root.culture
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = county.empire.holder
							exists = root.location.empire.holder
						}
						county.empire = root.location.empire
					}
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = county.kingdom.holder
							exists = root.location.kingdom.holder
						}
						county.kingdom = root.location.kingdom
					}
				}
				has_holding = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = piracy_destination_1
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				is_coastal = yes
				county.holder = { is_human = yes }
				NOR = {
					this = scope:piracy_destination_1
					culture = root.location.culture
					culture = root.culture
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = county.empire.holder
							exists = root.location.empire.holder
						}
						county.empire = root.location.empire
					}
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = county.kingdom.holder
							exists = root.location.kingdom.holder
						}
						county.kingdom = root.location.kingdom
					}
				}
				has_holding = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = piracy_destination_2
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				is_coastal = yes
				county.holder = { is_human = yes }
				NOR = {
					this = scope:piracy_destination_1
					this = scope:piracy_destination_2
					county.culture = root.culture
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = county.empire.holder
							exists = root.location.empire.holder
						}
						county.empire = root.location.empire
					}
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = county.kingdom.holder
							exists = root.location.kingdom.holder
						}
						county.kingdom = root.location.kingdom
					}
				}
				has_holding = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = piracy_destination_3
		}
	}

	option = { # Province 1

	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.9111.t

	theme = sailing_activity

		reference = ocean
	}

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = anger
	}
	right_portrait = {

		animation = marshal
	}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:first_mate = { is_alive = no }
			}
			set_first_mate = yes
		}
	}

	option = { # Attack
		name = agot_sailing.9111.a
		duel = {
			skill = martial
			value = very_high_skill_rating
			30 = { #Success
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 1.5
					min = -49
				}
				modifier = {
					has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
					add = 10
				}
				modifier = {
					has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					OR = {
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_mariner
							value >= 50
						}
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_mariner
							value < 100
						}
					}
					add = 20
				}
				modifier = {
					has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
					add = 40
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.9111.a.success
				send_interface_toast = {
					left_icon = root
					title = agot_sailing.9111.toast.a.success
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_activity_intent = gain_sailing_xp_intent
							OR = {
								NOT = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
								AND = {
									has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
									has_trait_xp = {
										trait = lifestyle_mariner
										value < 100
									}
								}
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
							add_trait_xp = {
								trait = lifestyle_mariner
								value = 5
							}
						}
						else = { add_trait = lifestyle_mariner }
					}
					else = {
						add_martial_lifestyle_xp = 100
						add_prestige = major_prestige_gain
						add_short_term_gold = major_gold_value
					}
					trigger_event = {
						id = agot_sailing.9114
						days = 15
					}
				}
			}
			70 = { #Failure
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				modifier = {
					has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
					add = -10
				}
				modifier = {
					has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					OR = {
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_mariner
							value >= 50
						}
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_mariner
							value < 100
						}
					}
					add = -20
				}
				modifier = {
					has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
					add = -40
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.9111.a.failure
				custom_tooltip = agot_sailing_end_failure
				scope:activity = {
					every_attending_character = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
							NOT = { this = scope:host }

					trigger_event = {
						id = agot_sailing.9002
						days = 15
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				has_trait = craven
				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = brave
				add = 20
			}
		}
	}

agot_sailing.9130 = { # Pirate ship encounter
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.9130.t
	desc = agot_sailing.9130.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = anger
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:dueled_pirate
		animation = marshal
	}
	trigger = {
		any_province = {
			is_coastal = yes
			county = {
				holder = { is_human = yes }
			}
			NOT = {
				culture = root.location.culture
				culture = root.culture
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						exists = county.empire
						exists = root.location.empire
					}
					county.empire = root.location.empire
				}
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						exists = county.kingdom
						exists = root.location.kingdom
					}
					county.kingdom = root.location.kingdom
				}
			}
			exists = this
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:first_mate = { is_alive = no }
			}
			set_first_mate = yes
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				is_coastal = yes
				county = {
					holder = { is_human = yes }
				}
				NOT = {
					culture = root.location.culture
					culture = root.culture
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = county.empire
							exists = root.location.empire
						}
						county.empire = root.location.empire
					}
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = county.kingdom
							exists = root.location.kingdom
						}
						county.kingdom = root.location.kingdom
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = selected_piracy_destination
		}
		create_character = {
			template = errant_soldier_character
			location = capital_province
			culture = scope:selected_piracy_destination.culture
			faith = scope:selected_piracy_destination.faith
			dynasty = none
			trait = lunatic_1
			save_scope_as = dueled_pirate
		}
	}

	option = { # Attack
		name = agot_sailing.9130.a
		duel = {
			skill = martial
			value = very_high_skill_rating
			30 = { #Success
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 1.5
					min = -49
				}
				modifier = {
					has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value < 50
					}
					add = 20
				}
				modifier = {
					has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					OR = {
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_mariner
							value >= 50
						}
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_mariner
							value < 100
						}
					}
					add = 40
				}
				modifier = {
					has_trait = lifestyle_mariner
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mariner
						value >= 100
					}
					add = 60
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.9130.a.success
				send_interface_toast = {
					left_icon = root
					title = agot_sailing.9130.toast.a.success
					add_martial_lifestyle_xp = 100
					add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
					add_dread = minor_dread_gain
					add_short_term_gold = major_gold_value
					trigger_event = {
						on_action = following_random_piracy_events_at_sea
						days = 15
					}

				if = {

							has_trait_xp = {

					if = {

							add_activity_log_entry = {
								key = agot_sailing_completed_log

								# this line below adds the entry to the Effects section of the conclusion UI
								show_in_conclusion = yes
								character = root

								root = {
									if = {
										limit = {
											has_trait_xp = {
												trait = lifestyle_mariner
												value < 100
											}
										}
										xp_modifiers = yes
									}
								}
							}

					}
					else = {

				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = greedy
				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = craven
				add = 20
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Release him!
		name = agot_sailing.9131.e

		send_interface_toast = {
			left_icon = root
			title = agot_sailing.9131.toast.e.success
			add_piety = minor_piety_gain
			if = {
				limit = { has_legitimacy = yes }
				add_legitimacy = minor_legitimacy_gain
			}

			stress_impact = {
				callous = medium_stress_impact_gain
				sadistic = medium_stress_impact_gain
				compassionate = medium_stress_impact_loss
				just = medium_stress_impact_loss
			}
			add_dread = minor_dread_loss
			add_gold = minor_gold_value
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			scope:dueled_pirate = { silent_disappearance_effect = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				has_trait = callous
				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = sadistic
				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = compassionate
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				has_trait = just
				add = 20
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		trigger_event = {
			on_action = following_random_piracy_events_at_sea
			days = 15
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random = {
				chance = 1
				trigger_event = agot_sailing.9140
			}
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.9140 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.9140.t
	desc = agot_sailing.9140.desc
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}

	right_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:first_mate
		animation = steward
	}

	trigger = {
		exists = house
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:first_mate = { is_alive = no }
			}
			set_first_mate = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.9140.a
		agot_sunken_treasure_map_effect = { OWNER = root }
	}
}

agot_sailing.9151 = { # Success
	type = activity_event
	title = agot_sailing.9151.t
	desc = {
		desc = agot_sailing.9151.desc
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_character_flag = sabotaged_production }
				desc = agot_sailing.9151.desc_sabotaged_production
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_character_flag = pillage_settlement }
				desc = agot_sailing.9151.desc_pillage_settlement
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_character_flag = duel_the_baron }
				desc = agot_sailing.9151.desc_duel_the_baron
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_character_flag = kidnap_villager }
				desc = agot_sailing.9151.desc_kidnap_villager
			}
		}
	}
	theme = sailing_activity
	override_background = {
		reference = ocean
	}

	right_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = happiness
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:host
		animation = aggressive_sword
	}
	center_portrait = {
		character = scope:local_villager
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:local_villager
			has_character_flag = kidnap_villager
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		add_gold = minor_gold_value
	}

	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.9151.a

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_trait = compassionate }
			}
			add_opinion = {
				modifier = impressed_opinion
				target = scope:host
				opinion = 20
			}
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.9500 = {
	scope = none
	hidden = yes
	trigger = {
		NOT = {
			exists = global_var:sunken_ship_variable
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = {
			name = sunken_ship_variable
			years = 4
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				is_coastal = yes
				county.holder = { is_human = yes }
			}
			save_scope_as = sunken_ship_province
		}
		scope:sunken_ship_province ?= {
			add_province_modifier = {
				modifier = sunken_ship_sighted
				years = 5
			}
		}
		every_player = {
			limit = { has_access_to_a_ship = yes }
			trigger_event = agot_sailing.9501
		}
	}
}

agot_sailing.9501 = {
	type = character_event
	title = agot_sailing.9501.t
	desc = {
		desc = agot_sailing.9501.desc_intro
		first_valid = { # Character's reaction
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_trait = sadistic }
				desc = agot_sailing.9501.desc_reaction_sadistic
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					NOT = {
						has_trait = sadistic
						has_trait = compassionate
					}
				}
				desc = agot_sailing.9501.desc_reaction
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_trait = compassionate }
				desc = agot_sailing.9501.desc_reaction_compassionate
			}
		}
		first_valid = { # Character's desire to loot the wreckage
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_trait = greedy }
				desc = agot_sailing.9501.desc_greed_greedy
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { scope:ship_province.county.duchy ?= title:d_tarth }
				desc = agot_sailing.9501.desc_greed_tarth
			}
			desc = agot_sailing.9501.desc_greed_not_tarth
		}
	}
	theme = sailing_activity

	right_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = shock
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:admiral
		animation = chancellor
	}

	trigger = {
		exists = cp:councillor_admiral
	}

	immediate = {
		scope:sunken_ship_province ?= { save_scope_as = ship_province }
		cp:councillor_admiral = { save_scope_as = admiral }
	}

	option = {
		name = agot_sailing.9501.a
		show_as_tooltip = {
			scope:ship_province = {
				add_province_modifier = {
					modifier = sunken_ship_sighted
					years = 5
				}
			}
		}

		stress_impact = {
			sadistic = miniscule_stress_impact_loss
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
}
